baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : yāmo vāsarāntyaḥ praharas tasya yamena uparameṇa hetunā mādhavas tat tad bālaka-vapus tat tat sva-gṛhaṁ gatas tan-mātṛṇair unmardanādibhiḥ sāyaṁ saṁlālita abhūd iti śeṣaḥ | unmardanaṁ sugandhi-tailābhyaṅganaṁ tato majjaḥ snapanaṁ śobhanair ācaritair māṭṝḥ praharṣayan ||23||

...

|| 10.13.24 ||

gāvas tato goṣṭham upetya satvaraṁ

huṅkāra-ghoṣaiḥ parihūta-saṅgatān |

svakān svakān vatsatarān apāyayan

muhur lihantyaḥ sravad audhasaṁ payaḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : gavāṁ mohanam āha—gāva iti | parihūtā āhūtāś ca te saṅgatāś ca tān ||24||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tathaiva gavām api snehādhikyam āha—gāva iti | parihūtena-āhvānena saṅgatān prāptān | muhur lihantya iti snehādikyam āha | ūdhaḥ stanādhikaraṇaṁ gavām avayavas tatra bhavam audhasam ||24||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : gavāṁ ca tathaiva sneha-viśeṣam āha—gāva iti | svakān svakān iti gavāṁ vatsalyātiśayena svabhāvataḥ | sva-putrasyaiva pāyanāt vatsatarān vṛddhiṁ gatān api vatsān audhasam āpīna-sambandhi, satvaram iti muhur iti, sravad ity-ādikaṁ cātrāpi pūrvato viśeṣo jñeyaḥ ||24||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : gavāṁ ca tathaiva sneha-viśeṣam āha—gāva iti | svakān svakān iti sveṣām eva nikaṭa-prāptatvāt | mamatā-sambandhanātiśayāc ca, vatsatarān vṛddhiṁ gatān api vatsān audhasam āpīnabharam iti | tadīya-sarva-payaḥ śravaṇārambhābhi-prāyeṇa tatrāpi satvaram iti muhur iti sravad | ity-ādikaṁ pūrvato viśeṣa-jñāpakam ||24||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : gopīnām iva tato gavām api mohanam āha—gāva iti | parihūtā ādāvāhūtas tataḥ saṅgatāś ca tān atrāpi satvaram iti | muhur lihantya iti muhuḥ sravad iti, snehādhikya sūcakam ||24||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : atha gavāṁ mohanam āha—parihūtā āhūtās tataḥ saṅgatāś ca tān ihāpi satvaram iti| muhur lihantyaḥ sravad iti pūrvataḥ snehādhikyaṁ sūcyate ||24||

...

|| 10.13.25 ||

go-gopīnāṁ mātṛtāsminn āsīt snehardhikāṁ vinā |

purovad āsv api hares tokatā māyayā vinā ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : etāvat tu vaiṣamyaṁ kṛṣṇasyāpi durnivāram āsīd ity āha—go-gopīnām iti | gavāṁ gopīnāṁ ca asmin kṛṣṇe mātṛtā upalālanādi-rūpā purovad evāsīt | kintu snehardhikāṁ vinā | snehādhikyaṁ tv idānīṁ viśeṣa ity arthaḥ | āsu go-gopīṣu hares tokatā ca bāla-bhāvanādi pūrvavad evāsīt | kintu māyayā vinā, mameyaṁ mātā aham asyāḥ putra iti mohaṁ vinā ity arthaḥ ||25||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : etāvad vakṣyamāṇam | vaiṣamyam āha—kiṁ ceti | ity artha iti—snehādhikyam eva vaiṣamyam iti bhāvaḥ | ity artha iti—māyāyāḥ svāśraya-mohakatvābhāvād iti bhāvaḥ |

nanu, śrīdāmādiṣu tan-mātṝṇāṁ yāvān snehas tāvān eva putrībhūte śrī-kṛṣṇe’pi bhavitum arhati | yāvac-chīla-guṇābhidhākṛti-vayaḥ [bhā.pu. 10.13.19] ity-ādy-ukteḥ | atrocyate—kṛṣṇo mahā-maheśvaratvāt svādhīnīkṛta-brahmādi-paryanto’pi premṇaḥ khalv adhīna eva | premā tu na tasyādhīna iti, premṇi tasya prabhutvābhāvāt tena premā saṅkucitīkartum aśakyam | ata eva svāmi-caraṇair api uktam | etāvat tu vaiṣamyaṁ kṛṣṇasyāpii durnivāram iti | sa ca premā vātsalyādi-rūpas tan-mātṛṣu virājata eva iti, kṛṣṇaḥ sva-mātrādi-samīpe svaiśvaryam anusandhāno’dhīnībhūta eva sadā tiṣṭhati, yathā cakravartinaḥ pārśve maṇḍaleśvaraḥ | na ca tasyaivaṁ pāratantryaṁ dūṣaṇam iti vācyam, pratyuta bhūṣaṇam eva | yathā jīvasya māyā-pāratantryaṁ duḥkhārthakaṁ tathaiveśvarasyānanda-rasamayasyāpi prema-pāratantryaṁ pratikṣaṇa-vardhamāna-niratiśayānandārthakam eveti mahānubhavair anubhūtam | śrī-yaśodāyāṁ tu śrī-kṛṣṇo’ham iti pratipādane’py āsu śrīdāmāham ity-ādi-cchadma-pratipādanān māyādhikyam eveti jñeyam ||25||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tat-tat-kāraṇaṁ tāsāṁ sneha-viśeṣam | tat-kāraṇaṁ ca tāsu śrī-bhagavat senha-viśeṣam āha—go-gopīnām iti | snehasya ṛddhikāṁ vṛddhim | anyat tair vyañjitam | tatra ca tokatā | pūrva-bālakavad-bālabhāvo vicitra bhojya-bhūṣaṇākrīḍanaka-prārthanādi-rūpo māyayā tāsū mamatā viśeṣātmikayā vinā pūrvaṁ tasya putratvābhāvena tāsu mamatā-viśeṣo nāsīt | idānīṁ ca putratvāpatyā sa jāta ity arthaḥ | evaṁ śrī-bhagavati tāsāṁ sneha-vṛddhis tāsu ca tasya putratvena mamatā-viśeṣo bhagavato'pi durnivāra iti, tātparyeṇa śrī-bhagavato māhātmya-viśeṣa eva sampadyeteti, athavā kāpaṭyaṁ vinā pūrva tāsu kiñcid vañcanam āsīt | adhunā ca śrī-yaśodāyām iva nijāśeṣa-rahasya prakāśanāt tatrāsīd ity arthaḥ |

yad vā, māyā dayā, tayā vinety adhunā tat tad vala-rūpasya śrī-bhagavatas tāsu sneho viśeṣato jāta ity arthaḥ | yad vā, akāra praśleṣeṇāmāyā kāpaṭyābhāvas tayā vinā iti pūrva-putrāṇāṁ tāsū kāpaṭyaṁ nāsīd, idānīṁ ca rasika-śiromaṇes tasya vicitra-dhūrtatā svabhāvena tāsāṁ sudmārtham iva tad abhūd ity arthaḥ ||25||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : evaṁ pūrvebhyao vatsa-pālebhyas tad-aṁśānāṁ vailakṣaṇyaṁ darśayaṁs tat-tad-bhāgyena ślāghyamānābhyo’pi tat-tad-rūpa-mātṛbhyo’pi tan-nija-rūpa-mātur vailakṣaṇyam āha—go-gopīnām iti | purovad ity ubhayatrāpy anvayaḥ | pūrvatra prāktana-bālakeṣv iva asmin bāla-vatsa-rūpaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇe mātṛtā mātṛ-bhāva āsīt | kiṁ tu sneharddhikāṁ vinā pūrveṣu sneha eva asmiṁs tu tat-samṛddhir ity arthaḥ | ācchanne’pi rūpe vastu-svabhāva-sthānāc chādyatvāt agnivat uttaratra purovat śrī-yaśodāyām iva āsv api śuddha-mātṛ-bhāvāsu hares tokatā bāla-bhāva evāsīn nānya-bhāva ity arthaḥ | kiṁ tu māyayā vinā tasyāṁ śrī-kṛṣṇo’ham iti satya-pratipādanam | tad-ucita-sākṣāt-kṛṣṇa-rūpa-prakāśanam | ca āsu tu sa śrīdāmāhaṁ sudāmāham iti chadma-pratipādanam | tad-ucita-rūpāntara-prakāśanaṁ cety arthaḥ | vakṣyate ca—vatsa-pāla-miṣeṇa sa [bhā.pu. 10.13.27] iti | sarva-vilakṣaṇasya prāpte’pi sāmye sarva-vilakṣaṇatā-hetu—svabhāva-viśeṣa-prāpty-asambhavāt īśvaravat atrobhayatrāpi samāna eva, vinā-śabdaḥ tataś cārtho’pi samāna eva yujyate | māyā-śabdasya ca svārtha eva sthitiḥ syāt—māyā dambhe kṛpāyāṁ ca iti viśva-prakāśāt | moha-vācitvaṁ tu lakṣaṇayaiveti āsv apy iti tatrāpy api-śabdena samāna-koṭi-niviṣṭāyāḥ śrī-yaśodāyāḥ prāptiḥ samañjasā syāt | anyathā harer apīty avakṣad iti vivecanīyam ||25||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : evaṁ pūrveśyo vatsa-bāleśyas tad-aṁśānāṁ vailakṣaṇyaṁ darśayan tat-tad-bhāgyena śalādhyamānābhyo’pi tat-tad-rūpa-mātṛbhyaḥ svarūpa-mātur vailakṣaṇyam āha—go-gopīnām iti | purovad ity ubhayatrāpy anveti, pūrvatra pūrva-bālakeṣv api uttaratra śrī-yaśodāyām ivety arthaḥ | tasyām ivāsv api śuddha-mātubhāvābhāvāsu hares tokatā tokabhāva evāsīt nānya-bhāvaḥ ity arthaḥ | kintu māyayā vinā tasyāṁ śrī-kṛṣṇa evāham iti satya-pratipādane tad ucita-sākṣāt kṛpṇa-rūpa-prakāśanañ ca āsu tu sa śrīdāmāhaṁ sudāmāham iti chadma-pratipādanaṁ tad-ucita-rūpāntara-prakāśanan cety arthaḥ | vakṣyate ca vatsa-pāla-miṣeṇa sa [10.13.27] iti ||25||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : kiṁ ca, gavāṁ gopīnām asmin śrī-yaśodā-nandena kṛṣṇe mātṛtā sarvā upalālanādimayaḥ sarva eva mātṛ-bhāva ity arthaḥ | purovat pūrvavad eva āsīt, kintu sneha-ṛddhikaṁ snehādhikyaṁ vinā pūrvaṁ śrīdāma-sudāmādibhyaḥ sva-putrebhyo'pi sakāśāt yaśodā-putre śrī-kṛṣṇe snehardhar āsīt | tasyaiva sva-putrī-bhūtatve jāte sati tadā sva-putreṣv api tathaiva sneha ṛdhir iti yaśodā-putre | sva-putre ca tulya eva sneho'bhūd ity arthaḥ | āsu go-gopīṣu harer api tokatā bāla-bhāvaḥ pūrvavad eva āsīt, kintu māyayā upaśareṇaiva putra-tulyatvāt putratvam āsīt | brahma-mohana-dinam ārabhya tu kṛṣṇe eva śrīdāma-sudāmādi-rūpas tāsāṁ putro'bhūd, iti kṛṣṇasya putra-bhāvo yathārtha evety arthaḥ |

nanu, śrīdāmādīṣu tan-mātṝṇāṁ yāvān snehas tāvān eva putrī-bhūte, kṛṣṇe'pi bhavitum arhati—yāvac chīla-guṇābhidhākṛti-vayo yāvad vihārādikam [bhā.pu. 10.13.19] iti pūrvokteḥ ucyate | kṛṣṇo hi mahā-maheśvaratvāt svādhīnī-kṛta-brahmādi-svāṁśa-paryanto'pi premṇaḥ khalv adhīna eva | premā tu na tasyādhīnaḥ iti, premṇi tasya prabhutvābhāvāt, premā saṅkucito-kartum aśakyaḥ | ata eva svāmi-caraṇair api uktam—etāvat tu vaiṣamyaṁ kṛṣṇenāpi durnivāram iti | sa ca premā vātsalyādi-rūpas tan mātrādiṣu virājaye iti | kṛṣṇaḥ sva-mātrādi-samīpe svaiśvarya-manana-sandhāno'dhīnībhūta eva sadā tiṣṭhati | yathā mahā-rāja-cakravartinaḥ samīpe maṇḍaleśvara iti, na ca mahā-maheśvarasya tasyaivaṁ pāratantryaṁ dūṣaṇam iti vācyaṁ, pratyuta bhūṣaṇam eva | yathā jīvasya māyā-pāratantryaṁ duḥkhārthakaṁ, tathaiveśvarasyānanda-rasamayasyāpi prema-pāratantryam | pratikṣaṇa-vardhamāna-niratiśayānandārthakam eveti mahānubhāvair anubhūtam ||25||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : pūrva-vṛttād gopīnāṁ mātṛ-bhāvāt hareḥ putra-bhāvāc cādhunikasya tasya tasya ca viśeṣam āha—go-gopīnām iti | gavāṁ gopīnāṁ ca asmin yaśodā-sute harau sarvā mātṛtā upapālanādimayo nikhilo mātṛbhāvaḥ purovat pūrvavad eva āsīt | kintu sneha-ṛdhikāṁ vinā iti pūrvaṁ śrīdāma-suvalādiṣu svasuteṣu sneha eva yaśodā-sute harau tu sneha ṛdhir idānīntu tasyaiva śrīdāmāditvenaika-vastuttvād ubhayatra prema ṛdhir ity arthaḥ | āsu gopīṣv api hare-stokatā bāla-bhāvaḥ pūrvavad eva āsīt | kintu māyayā vineti māyā syācchānvarī buddhor iti medinī | pūrva māsāṁ ca suto’ham iti buddi-racita eva tad-bhāvo na tu sākṣād idānīntu yaśodā-sutasyaiva śrīdāmādi-rūpatvāt sākṣāc ca sa ity arthaḥ ||25||

...

|| 10.13.26 ||

vrajaukasāṁ sva-tokeṣu sneha-vally ābdam anvaham |

śanair niḥsīma vavṛdhe yathā kṛṣṇe tv apūrvavat ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : sneha-rddhim eva darśayati—vrajaukasām iti | yathā kṛṣṇe yaśodā-nandane sva-putrebhyo’pi snehādhikyaṁ pūrvam āsīt | idānīṁ sva-tokeṣv api tathaivāvardhatety arthaḥ | ā abdaṁ yāvat-saṁvatsaram | niḥsīma yathā bhavati ||26||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : ity artha iti—sva-tokatvena pratīyamānasyāpi yaśodā-nandanatvād iti tātparyam | nirgatā sīmā maryādā yatra karmaṇi tat tatheti ||26||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : ata eva sarveṣāṁ pitṛ-mātrādīnāṁ bāla-rūpe tasminn eka-ābdaṁ nityam uttarottaram adhikaṁ sneha-bharo vyavardhatety āha—vrajaukasām iti | sneha eva vallī latā śanair nitya-vardhamānatvādinā, sva-śabdena nitya-tādṛśa-sneha-paramparā-vivṛddhi-vailakṣaṇyenāpi teṣu teṣāṁ, bhagavad-buddhir nābhūd iti dṛḍhīkriyate | śanair iti sneha-saṁlālana-kramāpekṣayā |

yad vā, ācchādanārtham anyathā parama-vailakṣaṇyena vitarkyatvād apūrvavad iti, asya anu aham ity anenānvayaḥ, vati-pratyayas tattvataḥ śrī-kṛṣṇe tasyāḥ svābhāvikatvena āścaryatvābhāvān niḥsīma yathā-syāt-tathā vavṛdha ity aparicchinnatā, anvaham iti nitya-vṛddhir api uktā | evam aparicchinnasyāpi nitya-nūtanatvena vṛddhyā premānandasya brahmānandato’py utkarṣaḥ sūcitaḥ | eṣa vaiṣṇava-siddhāntaḥ śrī-bhāgavatāmṛtottara-khaṇḍe vivṛto’sti ||26||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tadvac chrī-yaśodā-nandane’pi vailakṣaṇyam āha—vrajaukasām iti | sva-śabdena svāpatyatvenaiva jñāteṣv api, na tu śrī-kṛṣṇatvenety arthaḥ | sneha eva vallī śanair vardhamānatvādinā śanair iti | saṁlālana-kramāpekṣayā tad-aṁśatvāt teṣāṁ, kṛṣṇa ity ubhayatrāpy anu iti, yathā kṛṣṇe tathā teṣu vavṛdhe, kṛṣṇe tu apūrvavat pūrvaṁ yathā nāsīt, tathā vavṛdhe ity arthaḥ |

yad vā, yathā yathāvat teṣu kṛṣṇasya tat-tad-aṁśeṣu bālādi-śikya-paryanteṣu, tat-tad-yogyaṁ vavṛdhe kṛṣṇe tv apūrvavad aiśvarya-yuktaṁ vavṛdha ity arthaḥ | pūrvam api—yāvac-chīla-guṇābhidhākṛti-vayaḥ [bhā.pu. 10.13.19] ity anena sahaja-śrīdāmādivad eva śīlādyāvirbhāvas teṣūktaḥ, na tu śrī-kṛṣṇavad api | tataḥ svarūpādhikyam eva tatra premādhikye kāraṇaṁ jātaṁ, na tu śīlādyādhikyam apīty uttareṇa śrī-kṛṣṇe premādhikyaṁ tu durnivāram iti bhāvaḥ ||26||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : tadvat śrī-yaśodā-nandane’pi vailakṣaṇyam āha—vrajaukasām iti | kṛṣṇe ity ubhayatrāpy anu iti, yathā kṛṣṇe tathā teṣu vavṛddhe | kṛṣṇa tu apūrvavat pūrvaṁ, yathā nāsīt-tathā ity arthaḥ | yad vā, yathā yathāvat teṣu kṛṣṇasya tat-tad-aṁśeṣu bālādi-śikya-paryanteṣu tat-tad-yogyaṁ vavṛdhe | kṛṣṇe tv apūrvavad āścarya-yuktaṁ vavṛdha ity arthaḥ ||26||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : tad evaṁ yaśodā-nandana-kṛṣṇa-putrī-bhūta-kṛṣṇayoḥ svarūpata aikya-rūpāt snehādhikyam tulyam uktam api punaḥ spaṣṭī-kurvan yaśodā-nandana-kṛṣṇe tu guṇotkarṣa-hetukaṁ snehādhikyam āha—vrajaukasām iti | ābdaṁ varṣaṁ vyāpya anvahaṁ sneha-vallī iti, vallī yathā pratidinam eva vardhate, tathaivety arthaḥ | yathā kṛṣṇe yaśodā-nandane pūrvaṁ sva-putrebhyo’pi vardhamānā sā āsīt | idānīṁ sva-tokeṣu api tathaiva vavṛdhe iti | sva-tokānām api kṛṣṇatvāt sneha-ṛddhir ubhayatra tulyaivety arthaḥ |

kiṁ ca, tu-śabda-balāt kṛṣṇam ity asyāvṛttyā sneha-rddhes tulyatve’pi kṛṣṇe yaśodā-nandana-kṛṣṇe tu tathāpi apūrvavat | nitya-navāyamānaiva tasya sarva-śakti-saundarya-vaidagdhyādi-guṇavattvād aṁśitvāc ca, tāsāṁ putrībhūta-kṛṣṇa-svarūpāṇāṁ tu śrīdāmādy-ucita-saundaryādimattvād aṁśatvāc ceti bhāvaḥ | yad vā, yatheti yathāvad eva sneha-vallī vavṛdhe | kṛṣṇe tu apūrvavad eva vavṛdhe ity āvṛttyā vinaiva vyākhyeyam ||26||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : ubhayatra samānām eva sneha-rddhim uktāṁ sphuṭayann anācchanna-rūpe yaśodā-sute viśeṣam āha—vrajaukasām iti | ābdaṁ varṣaṁ vyāpyānvahaṁ pratidinaṁ sva-tokeṣu kṛṣṇāvirbhāveṣu śrīdāmādiṣu sneha-vallī vavṛdhe yathā kṛṣṇe yaśodā-sute śanair upalālanādi-krameṇa tu śabdād āvṛtyā vyākhyāyate | kṛṣṇe tv apūrvavan nitya-navāyamānaiva vavṛdhe | tasyāṁśitvād anācchanna-rūpa-guṇatvāc ca pare tv āhus tv āvṛtiḥ yathā yathāvad eva sneha-vallī vavṛdhe kṛṣṇe tv apūrvavad iti ||26||

...

|| 10.13.27 ||

ittham ātmātmanātmānaṁ vatsa-pāla-miṣeṇa saḥ |

pālayan vatsapo varṣaṁ cikrīḍe vana-goṣṭhayoḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : evam ātmā śrī-kṛṣṇo vatsapo bhūtvā tatrāpi vatsānāṁ pālānāṁ ca miṣeṇa ātmānam ātmanā pālayan krīḍitavān ity arthaḥ ||27||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : itthaṁ pūrvokta-rītyā | tatrāpi vatsapatve’pi | miṣeṇa chadmanā | ity artha iti—krīḍāṁ cakra ity evārthaḥ ||27||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : sa vatsa-vatsapa-rūpadharo vana-goṣṭhayoḥ śrī-vṛndāvane vraje ceti, tatra tatra kutrāpi kenacid api tad-hitaṁ nābhūd iti bhāvaḥ | anyat tair vyākhyātam | yad vā, sa kṛṣṇa ātmā svayam evaikākī cikrīḍe, svasyaiva tat tad rūpatvenānyābhāvāt ||27||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : sa vatsa-vatsapa-rūpa-dharaḥ vana-goṣṭhayor iti, tatra kutrāpi kenacid api tad-ūhitaṁ nābhūd iti bhāvaḥ | anyat taiḥ | yad vā, sa śrī-kṛṣṇa ātmā dvitīya eva cikrīḍe ||27||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : evam ātmā kṛṣṇo vatsapo bhūtvā vatsānāṁ pālānāṁ ca miṣeṇa ātmānam ātmanā pālayan krīḍitavān ity arthaḥ ||27||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : ittham eva sa vatsapa ātmā vibhur-vijñānānanda-mūrtiḥ kṛṣṇaḥ vatsānāṁ pālānāṁ ca miṣena ātmānaṁ pālayan cikriḍe iti | kenacid ūhito nābhūd ity arthaḥ ||27||

...

|| 10.13.28 ||

ekadā cārayan vatsān sa-rāmo vanam āviśat |

pañca-ṣāsu triyāmāsu hāyanāpūraṇīṣv ajaḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : etāvat-paryantaṁ rāmasyāpi moha evāsīt | saṁvatsarānte tu kathañcij jñātavān iti darśayann āha—ekadā iti | pañca-ṣāsu pañcasu vā ṣaṭsu vā rātriṣu hāyanasyāpūraṇīṣu pūrakatayāvaśiṣṭāsv ity arthaḥ ||28||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : etāvat-paryantaṁ kiñcin-nyūnābda-paryantam | kathañcit bhagavat-sūcanena | ajaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ | hāyanasya varṣasya—hāyano’strī śarat-samāḥ ity amaraḥ | ity artha iti—abda-pūrteḥ pañcaṣā-rātribhyaḥ prāg iti tātparyam ||28||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : ramayati śrī-kṛṣṇādīn iti rāmaḥ | ato yadyapi tena saha prāyo nityaṁ vane praveśas tathāpy ekadā sa-rāma iti sneha-viśeṣeṇa taṁ prati tadā tat-tad-rūpa-dhāraṇaṁ tattva-bodhanāpekṣayānyathā abdānte śrī-brahmāgamane sati tat-tat-sañcaraṇena tasya tat-kautukānubhavāsiddhiḥ | yad vā, saṅgopanārthaṁ tad-abde prāyo vane tasyānayanāt | vanam āviśat śrī-govardhanāntika-ghana-vanaṁ praviveṣety arthaḥ | vane sva-prabhāvasya svata eva viśeṣataḥ prākaṭyena tatraiva tena tad-yuktatvāt | laukika-garbhavan na jāyate, kintu svayam evāvirbhavatīty ajaḥ | ato garbhād anutpannatve'pi tasya putratā sambhaved iti bhāvaḥ | yad vā, na jāyate, na prakaṭo bhavatīty aja iti, tal-līlācchādanābhiprāyeṇa ||28||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : pañcaṣāsv ity aniścitoktiḥ prema-mohena | ramayati śrī-kṛṣṇādīn iti rāmaḥ | ataḥ prāyo yadyapi tena sahaiva praveśaḥ, tathāpy ekadā sa-rāma iti vṛttānta-viśeṣoddeśāya nirdeśaḥ | tac ca taṁ prati tal-līlā-tattva-bodhanam, sneha-viśeṣeṇa anyathā abdānte śrī-brahmāgamane sati tat-tat-saṁvatsareṇa tasya tat-kautukānubhava-siddheḥ | ataḥ pūrvam abodhane kāraṇaṁ tu vakṣyate—aja iti | yo vināpi janma tat-tat-putratāṁ prāptaḥ, sa ity arthaḥ | tat tv adhunā vyaktī-bhaviṣyatīti bhāvaḥ ||28||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : brahma-mohana-prasaṅga eva baladeva-mohanam api vyaktīkartuṁ kathām āha—ekadā iti | pañcasu ṣaṭsu vā rātriṣu hāyanasya varṣasya āpūraṇīṣu pūrakatayā avaśiṣṭāsv ity arthaḥ ||28||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : viriñca-vimohana-prasaṅge balabhadrasyāpi mohanaṁ vaktuṁ katham āha—ekadā iti | pañcasu ṣaṭsu vā tri-yāmāsu rātriṣu hāyanasya saṁvatsarasyāpūraṇīṣu pūrakatayāvaśiṣṭāsv ity arthaḥ ||28||

...

|| 10.13.29 ||

tato vidūrāc carato gāvo vatsān upavrajam |

govardhanādri-śirasi carantyo dadṛśus tṛṇam ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : tato dūrata eva govardhana-śikhare carantyo gāvo vraja-samīpe carato vatsān dadṛśuḥ ||29||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tatas tadā upavrajam iti | govardhaneśāna-koṇa-koṭi-sthāriṣṭa-mardana-kuṇḍa-samīpa-prāya-pradeśe | tad-antike vrajasya vyāpteḥ saṭīkarākhya-pradeśād ṛju-mārgeṇa catuḥ-krośa-mayatvāt | atha tarhy āgato goṣṭham ariṣṭo vṛṣabhāsuraḥ [bhā.pu. 10.36.1] iti vakṣyamāṇatvāc ca [vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī] ||29||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : upavrajam iti śrī-rādhā-devī-kuṇḍāntike vrajasya vyāptes tatas tadā | kiṁ vā, tebhyo vatsebhyo vidurād atidūre | yad vā, govardhanādrer vidurād upavrajaṁ carato vatsān tṛṇaṁ carantyo'pi dadṛśur iti snehādhikyaṁ bodhitam ||29||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tatas tad-anantaram upavrajam | govardhaneśāna-koṇa-koṭi-sthāriṣṭa-mardana-kuṇḍa-samīpa-prāya-pradeśe | tad-antike vrajasya vyāpteḥ saṭīkarākhya-pradeśād ṛju-mārgeṇa catuḥ-krośa-mayatvāt, atha tarhy āgato goṣṭham ariṣṭo vṛṣabhāsuraḥ [bhā.pu. 10.36.1] iti vakṣyamāṇatvāc ca | tṛṇam iti tat-prāśastya-vivakṣayā tad-āveśe’pi satīty arthaḥ | adhunā śrī-kṛṣṇena vatsatarāṇāṁ cāraṇaṁ kiñcid vayo-vṛddhyāyogyatvād iti jñeyam ||29||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : govardhana-śṛṅge tṛṇaṁ carantyo gāvaḥ tasmād avidūrāt vrajasya nikaṭe carato vatsān dadṛśuḥ ||29||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : govardhanasyādreḥ śirasi śikhare tṛṇaṁ carantyo gāvas tato’vidūrād vrajasyāntike carato vatsān dadṛśuḥ ||29||

...

|| 10.13.30 ||

dṛṣṭvātha tat-sneha-vaśo’smṛtātmā

sa go-vrajo’tyātmapa-durga-mārgaḥ |

dvi-pāt kakud-grīva udāsya-puccho

'gād dhuṅkṛtair āsru-payā javena ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : tadaiva sa go-vrajo gavāṁ kalāpas tat-sneha-vaśas teṣāṁ, vatsānāṁ snehenākṛṣṭo’ta eva na smṛta ātmā yena sa javena-upavrajam agāt | kathaṁ-bhūtaḥ | atyātma-pa-durga-mārgaḥ atikrānta ātma-pān gopālānyaḥ so’tyātma-paḥ, tathā durgamo mārgo yasya saḥ sa ca sa ca | tathā paraspara-yuktābhyāṁ padhyāṁ dhāvan dvi-pād iva pratīyamānaḥ | kakudy ākuñcitā grīvā yasya saḥ | udāsya-pucchaḥ unnamitānyāsyāni pucchāni ca yena saḥ | āsru-payāḥ sarvataḥ sravanti payāṁsi yasya saḥ | “āsnu-payāḥ” ity api pāṭhe sa evārthaḥ | go-vraja ity anena vṛṣabhādayo’py agur ity uktam ||30||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : sa govardhanam ūrdhva-gaḥ | upavrajam iti pūrvānuṣaṅgaḥ | kakudi kiñcid unnata-vṛṣa-skandhāvayave, kakuda strī kakudaś ca vṛṣāṅge rāḍ-dhvajevare iti medinī | vṛṣa upalakṣaṇaṁ gor api | ākuñcitā saṅkocitā | unnamitāny ūrdhvaṁ kṛtāni | prathame pāṭhe sru-gatau, dvitīye ṣṇu-prasravaṇe ||30||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tad evābhivyañjayati—dṛṣṭvā iti dvābhyām | tv-arthe cakāra pūrvato viśeṣāya | sa cākasmād akāṇḍe priya-sandarśana-svabhāvataḥ | kiṁ vā, tad dine'grajaṁ prati tal-līlā bodhanāya atha iti pāṭhe'pi sa evārthaḥ | sneha-vaśatvam eva darśayati asmṛta-ātmā ity-ādi viśeṣaṇaiḥ | ātmapān durga-mārgaṁ cātikrāntānto'tyātma-adurga-mārgaḥ ||30||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tad evābhivyañjayati—dṛṣṭvā iti dvābhyām | tv-arthe ca-kāraḥ pūrvato viśeṣāya sa cākāṇḍe priya-sandarśana-svabhāvataḥ | atha iti pāṭhe’pi sa evārthaḥ | sneha-viśeṣatvam eva darśayati—asmṛta-ātmā ity-ādibhiḥ | ātma-pān durga-mārgaṁ cātikrānto atyātmapa-durga-mārgaḥ astv ity-ādi-pāṭha-dvayam api kvib-antaṁ tug-abhāva ārṣaḥ ||30||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : asmṛta-ātmā ātmānam api vismṛtya, sa go-samūhaḥ agāt | atikrāntā ātmapā gopā durgā mārgāś ca yena saḥ | parasparaṁ yuktābhyāṁ padbhyāṁ dhāvan dvi-pād iva pratīyamānaḥ unmukhatvāt kakudi grīvā yasya sa | udgatānyāsyāni pucchāni ca yasya sa | ā samyag eva kṣaranti asrūṇi payāṁsi ca yasya saḥ ||30||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : sa go vrajo vatsān dṛṣṭvā agāt tān-agamat | kīdṛśa ? asmṛta-ātmā svam api vismṛtyety arthaḥ | atikrānto ātmapāḥ svarakṣakā gopā durgā maṛgāś ca yena saḥ | mitho yuktābhyāṁ padbhyāṁ dhāvan dvi-pād iva pratīyamānaḥ kakudi ākuñcitā grīvā yasya saḥ | unnatānyāsyāni pucchāni ca yasya saḥ | ā samyak sravanti payāṁsi netra-jalāni dugdhāni ca yasya saḥ ||30||

...

|| 10.13.31 ||

sametya gāvo’dho vatsān vatsavatyo’py apāyayan |

gilantya iva cāṅgāni lihantyaḥ svaudhasaṁ payaḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : tatra viśeṣato dhenūnāṁ ceṣṭitam āha—sametya iti | govardhanasyādhaḥ | vatsavatyaḥ punaḥ prasūtā api ||31||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tatra go-vraje lihantya āsvādayantyaḥ ||31||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : svakīyaṁ mahā-madhuratvādināsādhāraṇaṁ suśobhanaṁ vā, audhasam udhaumyaḥ sravad ity arthaḥ ||31||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : svīyaṁ mādhuryādibhir asādhāraṇaṁ suśobhanaṁ vā, odhasam ūdhobhyaḥ sravad ity arthaḥ ||31||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : govardhanasyādhaḥ sametya dvyāhikatryāhikādi-vatsavatyaḥ api audhasam ūdhobhyaḥ svayam eva sravat | payaḥ apāyayan gilantya iva iti, gavāṁ lehanādhikyaṁ snehādhikya-sūcakam ||31||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : gāvo govardhanādreradhaḥ sametya audhasam ūdhobhyaḥ sravat | payo vatsānapāyan vatsavatyaḥ punaḥ prasūtā āpe lihantya iti, gavāṁ lehanādhikyaṁ snehādhikyaṁ dyotayati ||31||

...

|| 10.13.32 ||

gopās tad-rodhanāyāsa-maughya-lajjoru-manyunā |

durgādhva-kṛcchrato’bhyetya go-vatsair dadṛśuḥ sutān ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : rāmeṇa dṛṣṭaṁ vatseṣu gavāṁ snehātiśayam uktvā gopānām api darśayitum āha—gopā iti | tāsāṁ gavāṁ rodhane ya āyāsas tasya maughyena moghatvena lajjayā sahoru-manyunā durga-mārga-janita-kleśena ca abhyetya govatsaiḥ saha sutān dadṛśuḥ ||32||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : rāmāya go-snehato’pi gopa-snehādhikyaṁ sūcayann āha—gopā iti ||32||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : anvetya paścād āgatya | pāṭhāntare'bhimukham etya ||32||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : abhyetya abhimukham etya anvetya iti kvacit pāṭhaḥ ||32||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : manyurgāḥ praty eva kṣeyaṁ na tu bālān prati gavām iva teṣāṁ, sa-bāleṣu darśana-sambhavād adarśane’pi vatsa-cāriṇāṁ teṣāṁ, mādhurya-sphūrtteś ca kopāsambhavāt ||32||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : tāsāṁ gavāṁ rodhane ya āyāso lakuṭotkṣepādibhis tasya maudhyena vaiyarthyena hetunā lajjā ca manyuś ca tal lajjāmanyu tena durga-mārga-janita-kleśena cābhyetya govatsaiḥ saha ||32||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : tāsāṁ gavāṁ rodhane ya āyāso, yaṣṭi-kṣepādis tasya maughyena vaiphalyena hetunā jāto, yo lajjā-yukto manyus tena durgādhva-janitena kṛcchreṇa ca abhyetya go-vatsaiḥ saha sutān dadṛśuḥ ||32||

...

|| 10.13.33 ||

tad-vīkṣaṇotprema-rasāplutāśayā

jātānurāgā gata-manyavo’rbhakān

uduhya dorbhiḥ parirabhya mūrdhani

ghrāṇair avāpuḥ paramāṁ mudaṁ te ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : tataś ca teṣām īkṣaṇenodgato yaḥ prema-rasas tasminn āplutā nimagnā āśayā yeṣāṁ te | lajjā-manyu-kṛcchrais tān api tāḍayitum āgatāḥ santo’pi gata-manyavaḥ pratyuta jātaḥ anurāgo yeṣāṁ te | mūrdhani ghrāṇair avaghrāṇaiḥ paramāṁ mudam avāpuḥ ||33||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tataḥ suta-darśanānantaram | gatam anyato vigata-krodhāḥ ||33||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) :na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : yady api āyāsādyaḥ prema-rasodayāntarāyā uktāḥ, tathā gavāṁ vatsaiḥ sahitān iti, vatsa-saṅge sthitair api sva-sva-putraiḥ parama-vatsalānāṁ tāsāṁ dṛṣṭi-pathe vatsānām ānayanād anapasāraṇāc ca teṣām aparādhaḥ sūcitaḥ | tathāpi teṣāṁ sutānām īkṣaṇena ya ut ucca utkṛṣṭo vā prema-rasas tasmin āplutāśayā udgṛhyoccair aṅke gṛhītvā udūhyeti pāṭhe dīrgham ārṣaḥ | arthaḥ sa eva | prema-rasānurāgayoḥ sahajatva-sopādhitvādinā hrāsa-rahitatva-rañjanatvādinā vā bhedaḥ kalpyaḥ | yad vā, pūrvam eva jātānurāgā adhunā ca tad-īkṣaṇena iti | yad vā, udgrahaṇādinā jāto’nurāga āsaktir yeṣāṁ, tathā-bhūtāḥ santo mudaṁ prāptāḥ, paramām iti mudaḥ parākāṣṭhoktā ||33||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : yady api āyāsādayaḥ prema-rasodayāntarāyā uktāḥ, tathā gavāṁ vatsaiḥ sahitān iti | vatsa-saṅge sthitair api sva-sva-sutaiḥ parama-vatsalānāṁ, tāsāṁ dṛṣṭi-pathe vatsānām ānayanād anapasāraṇāc ca, teṣām aparādhaḥ sūcitaḥ | tathāpi teṣāṁ sutānām īkṣaṇena ya utkṛṣṭaḥ prema-rasas tasmin āplutāśayā udgṛhya uccair aṅke gṛhītvā udgūhyeti pāṭhe dīrghatvam ārṣaḥ | arthaḥ sa eva | prema-rasānurāgayoḥ sukhātiśaya-tṛṣṇātiśayābhyāṁ viśeṣaṇābhyāṁ bhedaḥ kalpyaḥ | yadi ca gavām iva teṣām api dūrato’pi sva-suta-darśana-sambhavāt kathañcid darśanābhāve’pi vatsa-saṅgināṁ sva-sutānāṁ sphuraṇāt teṣu krodhotpattir na sambhavatīti śaṅkyate | tat tad āsv anātikrāmantīr gāḥ praty eva teṣāṁ, manyur mantavyaḥ sva-sutānām atisannihitatayā suṣṭhu mādhuryānubhaveṣu tac-chānti-pūrvaka-premodayaś ceti ||33||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : tad-īkṣaṇa iti prema-anurāgayor ullāsātiśaya-tṛṣṇātiśayābhyāṁ viśeṣaṇābhyāṁ bhedaḥ ||33||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : gata-manyava ity are anabhijñāḥ ! atra parama-vatsala-gavāṁ gaṇa-dṛṣṭi-pathe kathaṁ vatsā ānītāḥ ? iti tāṁs tāḍayituṁ-manaso’pi, teṣāṁ bālānām īkṣaṇodbhūtena prema-rasena āplutāśayās tataś ca jāta-anurāgāḥ premṇām eva pañcamīṁ kakṣām anurāgābhyāṁ tṛṣṇātiśaya-mayīṁ prāptāḥ gata-manyavaḥ vismṛta-krodhāḥ ||33||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : teṣāṁ vīkṣaṇādito yaḥ prema-rasas tenāplutāśayās tato jāta-anurāgāḥ premṇaḥ parāvasthām anurāgaṁ labdhvā gata-manyavo vismṛta-krodhāḥ santaḥ ||33||

...

|| 10.13.34 ||

tataḥ pravayaso gopās tokāśleṣa-sunirvṛtāḥ |

kṛcchrāc chanair apagatās tad-anusmṛty-udaśravaḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : kṛcchrāt tato’pagatā nivṛttāḥ | teṣāṁ sutānām anusmṛty udgacchanty aśrūṇi yeṣāṁ te ||34||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tatas tebhyaḥ pravayaso vṛddhāḥ ||34||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : pravayaso vṛddhā iti, teṣāṁ prāyo vivekitvādinā snehsa-rahasahāniḥ sūcitā | tathāpīti pūrvaṁ vṛddhā api kadācid-gopālā āsann iti bodhayati | ata evoktaṁ pravayasa iti vayo-bāhulyena vṛddhā na tu jarādy abhibhūtā ity arthaḥ | yad vā, prakṛṣṭaṁ vayo yeṣāṁ nitya-nava-yuvāna ity arthaḥ | anusmṛtir nirantara-smaraṇam ||34||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : pravayaso vṛddhāḥ iti, teṣāṁ prāyo vivekitvādinā tādṛg alpa-viyogena moho na sambhavati tathāpīty arthaḥ | evaṁ pūrvaṁ vṛddhā eva gopālā āsann iti bodhayati | tac ca gopa-jātes tat sva-dharmatvāt śrī-vrajeśasya ca bāla-putratvena sva-pratinidher abhāvena svayam eva gāḥ pālayataḥ | saṅgaucityāt anusmṛtiḥ nirantara-smaraṇam ||34||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : pravayaso vivekino ’pi ||34||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : pravayaso vṛddhāḥ kṛccrād eva śanair eva gocāraṇānurodhād eva apagatā tasmād āśleṣād viyujya gatās tataś ca vicchedotthayā, teṣām anusmṛtyā udgatāśravaḥ ||34||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : pravayaso bahvapatyā api te gopās tokānāṁ, teṣām āśleṣeṇa sunirvṛtāḥ kṛcchrād eva śanair eva gocāraṇānurodhena apagatās tad āśleṣād viyujya | gāḥ pratigatās, teṣām anusmṛtyā udgatāśravo’bhūvan ||34||

...

|| 10.13.35 ||

vrajasya rāmaḥ premardher vīkṣyautkaṇṭhyam anukṣaṇam |

mukta-staneṣv apatyeṣv apy ahetu-vid acintayat ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : premardher autkaṇṭhyam atiśayaṁ mukta-staneṣv apatyeṣu vīkṣya hetum ajānann acintayat ||35||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : anukṣaṇaṁ pratikṣaṇam | atiśayam ādhikyam ||35||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : premardher hetor anukṣaṇam autkaṇṭhyam āsaktim, autkaṭyam iti vā pāṭhaḥ, premardher ādhikyam ||35||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : premardher hetor autkaṇṭhya utkaṇṭhety upacārāt tatrātiśaya eva paryavasyati ṭīkāyām autkaṭhyam iti kvacit pāṭhaḥ ||35||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : vrajasya go-vrajasya apatyeṣu vatseṣu tad-upalakṣaṇatvena gopānāṁ bālakeṣv api jñeyam | eṣu mukta-stanyatva-viśeṣaṇānupayogāt premarddher hetor autkaṭyam ||35||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : premardher hetor autkaṇṭhyaṁ mukta-stanoṣv api vatseṣu nava-prasūta-vatsatarīṇām api gavām ahetu-vit hetum ajānan acintayad iti | etāvat kāleṣu pratidinam eva godohanādi-samayeṣu nava-prasūtān api vatsān vihāya prācīnān eva vatsān stanaṁ pāyantīḥ | sarvā eva gāḥ paśyato'pi tasya tasminn eva dine yac cintā prādur abhūt | tasminn api dine yad anyeṣāṁ pravayasāṁ vivekinām api gopānāṁ tathā cintanaṁ nābhūt | tatra-kāraṇaṁ yoga-māyaiva brahmā-mohanādinam ārabhyaiva go-gopī-gopānāṁ baladeva-sahitānāṁ sarveṣām eva bhagavatā sva-yoga-māyayā mohitatvāt | pratidina-virodha-darśane'pi virodhānusandhānaṁ na kasyāpy abhūt | kintu, sarva jagat kāraṇasya kāraṇārṇava-śāyino'pi paramāṁśitvena svāgrajatvena sva-priya-sakhatvena ca vañcanānaucityād | etal līlā-jijñāpayiṣā śrī-baladeve samucitāpi pūrvaṁ nābhūt | varṣa-paryantaṁ tat tac-chrīdāmādi-priya-sakha-viccheda-duḥkhasya tasmai dātum anucityāt | svasya tu tad-duḥkhaṁ nāsty eva, vatsa-kulānveṣakeṇaika-prakāśena tan-nikaṭa eva sthitatvāt | ato varṣāvasān eva bhagavataḥ sā tatra yadābhūt, tadā māyāpi śanaiḥ śanair aṁśenāṁśenaiva tasmād upararāma na tu yugapat | sāmastyena bhagavad aiśvarya-siddhau tam api bhaktābhimānāspadīkṛtya nimajjayitum ity avasīyate ||35||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : evaṁ gavāṁ gopānāṁ sva-tokeṣu ca ceṣṭāṁ vīkṣya balabhadrasya vimarśam āha—vrajasya iti | gopīnāṁ gavāṁ gopānāṁ ca mukta-staneṣv apy apatyeṣu premardhe-hetor autkaṇṭhyaṁ vīkṣya-ahetu-vit-tat-kāraṇam ajānann idam acintayat | yady api virañci-mohana-dinam ārabhyaivaṁ-virodho balabhadreṇa dṛśyata eva | tathāpi tad-dine tad anusandhānaṁ tasmin tal-līlā vijijñāpayiṣāyās tadaiva jātatvena sva-yoga-māyāyāḥ śanair apasāraṇād uktābhimāninas tasya svaiśvarya-siddhāvakasmād eva nimajjanīyatvād iti bodhyam ||35||

...

|| 10.13.36 ||

kim etad adbhutam iva vāsudeve’khilātmani |

vrajasya sātmanas tokeṣv apūrvaṁ prema vardhate ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : cintām evāha—kim etad iti dvābhyām | vāsudeve yathā purā prema, tathā sva-tokeṣv api vrajasya apūrvaṁ prema vardhate | kim etad adbhutam ? iti | kiṁ ca, sātmano mat-sahitasya vrajasya | mamāpy eteṣu premātiśayaḥ, tatra kāraṇaṁ kim ? iti bhāvaḥ ||36||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : atyadbhutam āha—kiṁ ca, | iti bhāva iti śuddha-sattva-svarūpe mayi mohaḥ kathaṁ jātaḥ ? iti, ahaṁ na jāne iti tātparyam ||36||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : adbhutaṁ yukty-atītam, akhilasya jagata ātmani paramātmani yato vāsudeve cittādhiṣṭhātari catur-vyūha-śreṣṭhe | yad vā, khilo nyūna ātmāpi priyatayā yasmāt, tato'py adhikataraḥ priya ity arthaḥ | kiṁ ca, vāsudeve nijāśeṣaiśvarya-prakaṭanārthaṁ śrī-vasudeva-putratāṁ prāpte | evaṁ sarvathā tasminn eva prema-vṛddher yogyatoktā, tathāpi tasminn iva tokeṣu bālakeṣu | yady api gavām tādṛśa eva sneho vatseṣūktaḥ, tathāpi tokeṣv iti-manuṣyāṇām eva sarveṣām evety arthaḥ ||36||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : adbhutaṁ yukty-atītam akhilasya ātmani paramātmani, ato vāsudeve sarvāśraye | kiṁ vā, tatrāpi śrī-vasudevāpatye pūrṇa-bhagavattayā prakaṭa ity arthaḥ | evaṁ sarvathā tasminn eva tad ucita-tādṛśa-prema-vṛddher yogyatoktā | tathāpi tasminn iva vrajasya go-gopa-ātmakasya tokeṣu svāpatyeṣu ||36||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : akhilasya-ātmani paramātmani ato vāsudeve sarvāśraya iti, tatraiva tādṛśa-prema-yogyatokta ||36||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : prathamaṁ māyāṁśoparame sati virodha-darśanotthaṁ tasya cintanam āha—kim etad iti | vāsudeva iva iti, vāsudeve yathā purā prema, tathā sva-tokeṣv api, vrajasya prema vardhate | kim etad adbhutaṁ, kiṁ ca, sātmanaḥ | mat-sahitasya mamāpi teṣu kṛṣṇavat prema kim ity arthaḥ ||36||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : yoga-māyāyāḥ prathamāṁśāpasaraṇe sati virodha-darśanajaṁ tasya cintanam āha—vrajasya sātmano | mat-sahitasyāpi yathā vāsudeve kṛṣṇe premāsīt, tathā tokeṣv api tad-vardhate etad adbhutam āścaryaṁ kiṁ mama tokeṣu premā kṛṣṇe tu tad atiśayo yogya iti bhāvaḥ ||36||

...

|| 10.13.37 ||

keyaṁ vā kuta āyātā daivī vā nāry utāsurī |

prāyo māyāstu me bhartur nānyā me’pi vimohinī ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : keyaṁ māyā devānāṁ vā narāṇāṁ vā asurāṇāṁ kuto vā kasmāt prayuktā ? tatrānya-māyā na sambhavati | yato mamāpi moho vartate, ataḥ prāyaśo mat-svāminaḥ śrī-kṛṣṇasyaiva māyā iyam astv iti sambhāvayati ||37||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : sambhāvayati—kalpayati | yadyapi śrī-baladevasya kāraṇārṇavaśāyitvena paramāṁśitvena tātkālika-jyeṣṭhatvena vañcanānaucityaṁ, tathāpi yāvad-abdaṁ śrīdāmādi-priya-sukha-viccheda-duḥkha-dānam anucitaṁ matvā pañcaṣa-rātra-śeṣe eva tat tasmai prābodhayat | tadaiva māyāpi śanair aṁśāṁśenopararāma | prathamāṁśoparame kim etad ? iti, acintayat saḥ | dvītīyāṁśoparame māyeyaṁ punar āha—daivī iti | devā brahmādyā aiśvarya-parīkṣaṇārthaṁ kiṁ vatsa-pālā bhūtvāsmākaṁ cittaṁ sveṣu snehayanti | naite śrīdāmādyāḥ narā ṛṣy-ādayaḥ. kim asmaj-jñāna-parīkṣaṇārthaṁ vatsādyā abhūvan ? kiṁ vā, asurāḥ kaṁsādyā eva balena apārayantaḥ chalenāsmākaṁ hiṁsanārtham ete’bhūvan ? tatas tṛtīya-māyāṁśoparame sati prāya ity-ādy āha | śrī-kṛṣṇasyaiveyaṁ yoga-māyās tv iti caturthāṁśasyāpy uparame sati kṛṣṇa-rūpān eva tān apaśyad ity āha |

yā vā, prāyo daivī nāṛy āsurī vānyā vā māyās tu bhartuḥ sarva-pālakasya me keyaṁ vimohinī api tu neti yojyam ||37||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : daivī devair jagat-pūjyaiḥ śrī-garūḍādibhir bālakānāṁ tat-tad-avatāratvena teṣu sneha-viśeṣāpekṣayā kṛtā, śrī-kṛṣṇaika-parāṇāṁ teṣām | sā na sambhavatīti pakṣāntaraṁ kalpayati—nārī iti | śrī-vasudevādibhiḥ śrī-nandādibhir vā kṛtā, anyathā sarveṣāṁ bālānāṁ śrī-kṛṣṇa-viṣayakerṣyotpattiḥ | bālānāṁ svābhāvika-śrī-kṛṣṇa-viṣayaka-sneha-bharam ālocyerṣyā-sambhavāt | pakṣāntaraṁ kalpayati—uta vā, āsurī vā, sva-svāpatyeṣv api śrī-kṛṣṇa-sadṛśa-sneha-vivardhanena vrajasya śrī-kṛṣṇa-viṣayaka-bhāva-viśeṣa-hānyā tan-māhātmya-saṅkocādy-arthaṁ kaṁsādibhiḥ kṛteti | tathā tu śrī-kṛṣṇa iva teṣu mama sneha-vṛddhir na sambhavatīty āha—prāya iti | tasya sva-viṣayaka-vañcanāsambhāvanāyā hetv-anālocanayā vā | astu syād anirdhāraṇe sambhāvanā | yad vā, astu bhavaty anyathā mayayā man-mohanena mal-lajjādy-utpattiḥ | vi-śabdena dīrgha-kālatvādy-apekṣayā ||37||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : athātra kāpi kasyāpi māyaiva hetur bhaved iti tarkayati—keyam iti | iyaṁ teṣu prema-vardhinī māyā durghaṭa-ghaṭanī śaktiḥ, kā kiṁ-lakṣaṇā ? vā-śabdaḥ samuccaye | kuta āyātā kasmāt samudbhūtā ? kena ca kṛtā ? ity arthaḥ | kutaḥ ? ity eva vicārayati | vā-śabdo vitarke | tat-tat-pitrādy-upāsitair devaiḥ kair api mahā-prabhāvaiḥ kṛtā kiṁ ? tebhyo’pi munīnāṁ prabhāvaṁ paryālocya tathaiva pakṣāntaraṁ kalpayati—nārī iti | atrāpi vā-śabdo yojyaḥ |

nanv evaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇavan nija-putrādiṣu vraja-janānāṁ prema-vardhana-spardhā ca na sambhavati ity āśaṅkya punar vikalpayati—uta pakṣāntare āsurī sva-svāpatyeṣv api śrī-kṛṣṇa-sadṛśa-sneha-vivardhanena vrajasya śrī-kṛṣṇa-viṣayaka-bhāva-viśeṣa-hānyā | tan-māhātmya-saṅkocādy arthaṁ kaṁsādibhiḥ kṛtā kiṁ ? pūtanādīnāṁ tan-mohanatā darśanāt | yad vā, devānāṁ munīnāṁ ca tal-līlā-lobhena prācīnānantardhāpya svayam āvirbhāvamayī sā tu teṣāṁ, sādhūnāṁ na sambhavatīti | tarkāntare asurāṇāṁ tu pūtanā-vatsāsurādivad duṣṭa-bhāvamayīti jñeyam | tayā tu śrī-kṛṣṇa iva teṣu mama sneha-vṛddhir na sambhavatīty āha—prāya iti | tasya sva-viṣayaka-vañcanā-sambhāvanāyā hetv-anālocanayā tādṛśa-premṇas tat-svarūpaikānuvadhyatālocanayā ca prāya ity uktam | astu syāt anirdhāraṇe sambhāvanā | yad vā, astu avatv iti prārthanā | anyathā māyayā man-mohanena mal-lajjādy-utpatteḥ, vimohinī niranusandhāna-prema-vardhinī vi-śabdo dīrgha-kālatvādy-apekṣayā iti lakṣaṇam apy asyāḥ darśitam ||37||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : keyam iti | astv iti sambhāvanā | prāya iti tādṛśa-premṇas tat-svarūpaikānubandhyatvāt tan-māyāyā apy asambhāvanā ||37||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : evaṁ tasya sarva-mayatve vatsapa-vatsakānāṁ mātrādeḥ sva-sva-tanayaṁ prati tat-samānā prītir evābhūt, na tu tad-dhetu—jñānam | śrī-baladevasya tu cireṇaivābhūd iti | tad uktim āha—prāyo māyāstu me bhartur [bhā.pu. 10.13.37] ity-ādi | me mama bhartur īśvarasyeyaṁ māyā | prāyaḥ-śabdo’numiti-param | tatrāha—me api mamāpi vimohinī | ato nānyā nānyasya māyā yayāham evam etāvantaṁ kālaṁ mohita evāsam | tasmān mad-bhartur eveti sva-svāṁśatvaṁ svayam eva prakaṭitam ||37-43||

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : bhavatu sarvajñatayaiva kāraṇam asya jñāsyāmīti | kṣaṇaṁ parāmṛśya dvitīya-māyāṁśoparame sati māyā iyam iti niścitya sā | kīdṛśī kutastyā kiṁ sambandhinīti ? punar vitarkayati—keyaṁ māyā hetoḥ kuto deśād vā ? daivī iti devā brahmādyā eva kim ? aiśvarya-parīkṣaṇārthaṁ vatsa-bālakā bhūtvā asmākaṁ cittaṁ sveṣu snehayanti | naite śrīdāmādyāḥ nārī iti narā ṛṣy-ādaya eva | kiṁ jñāna-parīkṣārtham ete vatsādyā abhūvan ? āsurī ity asurā kaṁsādaya eva kiṁ ? balenāpārayantaś chalenāsmākaṁ hiṁsārtham ete'bhūvann iti bahudhā vikalpya tṛtīyamāṁśoparame sati punaḥ sambhāvayati—prāya iti | me bhartuḥ śrī-kṛṣṇasyaiva māyā iyaṁ mahā-yoga-māyākhyā śaktir asādhāraṇī, yasyāḥ khalu mayā-niyantṛtvam asmāsu viśuddha-ghana-citsv apy adhikāraḥ astv iti | sambhāvanāyāṁ loṭ | nānya iti | kā nāma sā māyā ? mamāpi mohinī yato mad-aṁśasya mahat-sraṣṭuḥ puruṣasyāpi māyayā brahmādikaṁ sarva-jagan-mohitam iti bhāvaḥ ||37||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : astu sārvajñyenaitat-kāraṇaṁ jñāsyāmīti | kṣaṇaṁ vimṛśya dvitīyāṁśāpasaraṇe sati māyā iyam iti nirdhārya, sā ca kiṁ-lakṣaṇā ? kutastyā ? kiṁ-sambandhinī ? iti punar vitarkayati—keyaṁ māyā kuto hetor āyātā daivī devānāṁ vā iyaṁ viriñcādayo’smad-aiśvarya-parīkṣāyai vatsa-vatsapā bhūtvā sveṣv asmac-cittaṁ snehayantīty arthaḥ | nārī narāṇāṁ vā iyaṁ, ṛṣayo vā asmaj-jñāna-parīkṣāyai vatsapā abhūvan ity arthaḥ | uta āsurī asurāṇāṁ veyaṁ kaṁsādaya eva balenāsmān jetum asamarthāś chalena hiṁsituṁ tathā bhavann iti bahudhā vikalpya | tṛtīyāṁśāpasaraṇe sati punaḥ sambhāvayati—prāya iti, me bhartuḥ svāminaḥ kṛṣṇasyaiveyaṁ yogakhyā māyā bhavati, anyā triguṇā māyā kṛṣṇa-vaimukhyād eva hetor āyātā sā mamāpi vimohinī naḥ bhavati | mad-aṁśasya kāraṇodaśayasya mahat-sraṣṭur māyayā viriñcādi-pramukhaṁ jagad-vimohitam astīti | devī-nāry-āsurī ca māyā mad-vimohane kutaḥ prabhavet ? tasmān mad-bhartur eva sā astv iti, sambhāvanāyāṁ loṭ ||37||

...

|| 10.13.38 ||

iti sañcintya dāśārho vatsān sa-vayasān api |

sarvān ācaṣṭa vaikuṇṭhaṁ cakṣuṣā vayunena saḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : sa-vayasān sakhīn api vayunena jñāna-mayena cakṣuṣā vaikuṇṭhaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇam evāpaśyat ||38||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : sa rāmaḥ | vayunena samāhita-jñāneneti viśvanāthaḥ ||38||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : vaikuṇṭhaṁ sarvathā kuṇṭhatā-rahitam iti, tat-tad-rūpa-samyaktābhipretā | vayunena cakṣuṣāntar-dṛṣṭyety arthaḥ | tathāpy asambhavam āśaṅkya pariharati—dāśārhaḥ śrī-yadukulodbhavo bhrātṛtvaṁ gata ity arthaḥ ||38||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : sa-vayasān iti samāsānta ārṣaḥ | vaikuṇṭhaṁ svārthe’ṇ sarvathā kuṇṭhatā-rahitam iti | tad rūpate liṅgaṁ vayunena anusandhānātmaka-jñāna-mayena tasya vayunasya prema-viśeṣamayatvena sāmartha-viśeṣaṁ dyotayati | dāśārhaḥ śrī-yadukulodbhavaḥ bhrātṛtvaṁ gata ity arthaḥ ||38||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : sa-vayasān iti samāsānta ārṣaḥ | daivīty-ādivat svayaṁ sambhāvya nirasyati ||38||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : bhavatu samādhāya jñāna-dṛṣṭyā punar api etān nibhālayāmīti | vicāre sati caturtha-māyāṁśasyāpi śrī-kṛṣṇasyecchayaivoparame sati tān yathārthān kṛṣṇa-svarūpān etān apaśyad ity āha—sa-vayasān iti | samāsānta ārṣaḥ | vayunena samāhita-jñānamayena cakṣuṣā vaikuṇṭhaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇam evāpaśyat ||38||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : asty evaṁ punar api samādhitā yathāvan nibhālayāmīti vimarśe sati caturthāṁśasyāpasaraṇe sva-bhartuḥ svarūpān eva tān vatsān vatsapāṁś cāpaśyad ity āha—iti sañcintya iti | vayunena cakṣuṣā samāhita-jñānamayena netreṇa sarvān vaikuṇṭhaṁ kṛṣṇam ācaṣṭa dṛṣṭavān | sa-vayasān ity ārṣaḥ | samāsāntaḥ balabhadrasyeśvarasyāpi kṛṣṇa-svāṁśatvāt tad-icchā-jñānādīnāṁ tad-aṁśi-kṛṣṇa-niyamyatvād idaṁ sarvaṁ saṅgatimad iti tattva-vidaḥ ||38||

...

|| 10.13.39 ||

naite sureśā ṛṣayo na caite

tvam eva bhāsīśa bhidāśraye’pi |

sarvaṁ pṛthak tvaṁ nigamāt kathaṁ vade-

ty uktena vṛttaṁ prabhuṇā balo’vait ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : evaṁ kṛṣṇa-rūpān sāmānyato jñātvā kṛṣṇaṁ pṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇopadeśena viśeṣato jñātavān ity āha—na eta iti | ayam arthaḥ—pālyamānās tāvad vatsā ṛṣīṇām aṁśāḥ, pālāś ca devānām aṁśā iti, tāvad ahaṁ vedmi | idānīṁ tu na tathā, kintv asmin bhid-āśraye’pi tvam eva bhāsi | atas tvaṁ sarvaṁ pṛthak vivicya, kathaṁ vṛttam ? iti vada ity uktena prabhuṇā vaktrā nigamāt saṅkṣepata evoktam | vṛttaṁ balaḥ avaid viditavān ity arthaḥ ||39||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : jñātvāpi siddhāntaṁ kartuṁ śrī-kṛṣṇaṁ praty āha—na ete iti | ayam artha iti | asyāyaṁ bhāva ity arthaḥ |

vatsāḥ sarve gokule’tra ṛṣe ṛṣaya eva hi |

gopāḥ sarve deva-rūpā jātā brahmājñayā dhruvam || ity-ādi-purāṇāt |

tāvat sakalam yāvat tāvac ca sākalye ity ukteḥ | iha tv anyathaiva bhātīty āha—kintv iti | bhid-āśraye gopa-vatsa-dehe | yatas tvam eva bhāsi ato hetoḥ | kathaṁ vṛttam etat ? kathaṁ jātam ? iti | ity ukte baladevenettham uktena | prabhuṇā kṛṣṇena | ity arthaḥ iti | vitter bahv-arthatvād atra jñānārthatā jñeyeti bhāvaḥ | yad vā, nigamād vedāt | sarvaṁ khalv idaṁ brahma [chā.u. 3.14.1] ity evaṁ-rūpāt vivicya vicārya sarva-dṛśyādṛśyaṁ pṛthak katham iti tvaṁ vada | ity uktena ittham uktavatā satā prabhuṇā balo vṛttaṁ tātkālikaṁ brahmaṇe nija-vaibhava-darśana-rūpam avaid iti |

[viśvanātha-tīkānusāreṇa] tataś ca kṛṣṇasyaivaṁ vatsa-bālakī-bhāve kiṁ kāraṇaṁ prayojanaṁ ca te ? vatsa-bālā vā kva gatāḥ ? iti | bahutara-samādhināpi yat svayaṁ jñātuṁ neṣṭe, tatra māyā na kāraṇaṁ, kintu svayaṁ bhagavataḥ kṛṣṇasya khalv aiśvaryam asādhāraṇam itthaṁ svarūpam eva sarvatra sarvajñā api nārāyaṇādayaḥ parameśvarāḥ svāṁśā api yad-viṣayakam alpajñatvam eva bibhrati, na tu svarvajñatvaṁ svata iti | atra pramāṇaṁ dvārakā-vāsi-brāhmaṇa-bāla-hartā bhūmā mahā-puruṣo’py agre ākhyāsyate | tasmāc chrī-baladevaḥ kṛṣṇaṁ dṛṣṭvaiva sarvaṁ tattvam avagatavān ity āha—na eta iti |

sureśā brahmādyā eva māyayā vatsa-bālākārā ete na sambhavanti, nāpi ṛṣayaḥ | cān nāpy asurāḥ kintu bhid-āśraye’pi vividha-bhedāspade’pi vatsa-bālādi-samūhe tvam eva eko bhāsi | ekasyāpi tava pṛthaktvaṁ vatsa-pālādi-ṛūpatvaṁ kathaṁ ? tan nigamāt saṅkṣepato vadety uktena pṛṣṭena prabhuṇā śrī-kṛṣṇena hetunā balo’vait, brahma-mohanādi-vṛttaṁ jñātavān ||39||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : sureśā deva-śreṣṭhāḥ śrī-garuḍādayaḥ, ṛṣayaḥ śrī-vaśiṣṭhādyāḥ | vicitra-śrī-matsyādi-rūpe'vatāratvenābheda-viṣaye tvaṁ bhāsy eva, bhid-āśraye vatsa-bālakādi-rūpa-vividha-bhedasyāśraye'pi vastunīty api-śabdārthaḥ | eva-kāreṇa tad-eka-rūpatayā sureśatvādikaṁ nirastam | anyat tair vyākhyātam |

yad vā, ete vatsādayo na sureśās tat-tat-krīḍā-lobhena śrī-brahmādayo vatsādi-rūpāḥ santīti nety arthaḥ | svata eva teṣāṁ tatrāsamarthyena pakṣāntaram āha—na vai ete ṛṣaya iti śrī-nāradādyāḥ | kintu tvam eva vatsādi-rūpeṇa bhāsi, ata eko'pi tvaṁ pṛthag vividha-bhedena vartamānaṁ sarvam idaṁ vatsādi-rūpaṁ kuto'bhūḥ ? iti nigamād vadeti snehena vāk-śramo nirastaḥ | yad vā, nigamāt pṛthak vedātītam ity arthaḥ | sarveṣām api sac-cid-ānanda-ghana-rūpatvāt tatra ca vividha-bhede'py abhedāt | prabhuṇā mādṛśām tasyaiva vā īśvareṇa hetunā vṛttaṁ tad ajñāsīt | yato balaḥ sarva-sāmarthyādhikyavān, balādhikyād-balaṁ viduḥ [bhā.pu. 10.8.12] ity ukteḥ | evaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇānugraheṇaiva tad-vijñānaṁ bodhitam | anyat samānam |

etāvantaṁ kālaṁ tasya tattva-jñānaṁ tal-līlā-nirvāhāya śrī-bhagavad-icchayaiva, anyathā dayālu-sarala-svabhāvasya tasya priyatamānāṁ tādṛśāvasthā-sahanāśaktiḥ | kiṁ vā, sahaja-bhaktyā navīṇeṣu teṣu śrī-kṛṣṇa iva vyavahāreṇa sarveṣāṁ tat-tattva-vitarkyatvāpattir iti dik ||39||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : sureśā deva-śreṣṭhāḥ śrī-garuḍādayaḥ | ṛṣayaḥ śrī-nāradādyāḥ | bhid-āśraye’pi bāla-vatsādi-samūho’yaṁ yadyapi vividha-bhedasyāśrayaḥ, tathāpi tasminn ity arthaḥ | tvam eva bhāsi iti svarūpānandādi naikyānubhavāt | anyat taiḥ |

yad vā, daivī vā nāry utāsurī [bhā.pu. 10.13.37] itivad vitarkya pariharati—na eta iti | ete vatsādayo na sureśā na ca ṛṣayaḥ te tat-tat-krīḍā-lobhena tān antardhāpya vatsādi-rūpāḥ santīti nety arthaḥ | teṣām īdṛśa-premāspadatvābhāvāt, itthaṁ satāṁ brahma-sukhānubhūtyā [bhā.pu. 10.12.11] ity-ādi-rītyā, tādṛk kṛta-puṇya-puñjatvābhāvāc ca āsurīti | asyottara-pakṣe tad-ullekhas tu saṅkocān na kṛtaḥ, ata eko’pi tvaṁ pṛthag vividha-bhedena vartamānaṁ sarvam idaṁ vatsādi-rūpaṁ kuto’bhūḥ ? iti nigamād vada | iti snehena vāk-śramo nirastaḥ | prabhuṇā mādṛśāṁ tasyaiva ceśvareṇa hetunā vṛttaṁ tad [avait] ajñāsīt, yato balaḥ sarva-sāmarthyādhikyavān, balādhikyād balaṁ viduḥ [bhā.pu. 10.8.12] ity-ukteḥ | evaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇasyānugraheṇaiva tad-vijñānam bodhitaṁ | anyat samānam |

etāvantaṁ kālaṁ tasya tat-tattvājñānaṁ tal-līlā-nirvāhāya śrī-bhagavad-icchayaiva | sā ca dayālu-sarala-svabhāvasya mad-agrajasya teṣāṁ tādṛśāvasthā-sahanaṁ na syād ity āśaṅkyaiva ||39||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : naita iti | itthaṁ satām [bhā.pu. 10.12.11] ity-ādi-rītyā teṣāṁ tādṛśa-kṛta-puṇya-puñjatvābhāvāt bhid-āśraye’pi yadyapi bhid-āśraye’yaṁ vatsādi-samūhaḥ, tathāpīty arthaḥ ||39||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : tataś ca kṛṣṇasyaivaṁ vatsa-bālakī-bhāve kiṁ kāraṇam ? kiṁ vā, prayojanam ? te vatsa-bālakā vā kva sthāpitāḥ ? iti bahutara-samādhināpi tat svayaṁ jñātuṁ neṣṭe | tatra māyā na kāraṇaṁ, kiṁ tu svayaṁ bhagavataḥ kṛṣṇasya khalv aiśvaryam asādhāraṇam itthaṁ svarūpam eva sarvatra sarvajñā api nārāyaṇādayaḥ parameśvarāḥ svāṁśā api yad-viṣayakam alpajñatvam eva bibhrati, na tu sarvajñatvam | svata ity atra pramāṇaṁ dvārakā-vāsi-vipra-bālaka-hartā bhūmā mahā-puruṣo'py agrata ākhyāsyate, tasmāt śrī-baladevaḥ kṛṣṇaṁ dṛṣṭvaiva sarvaṁ tattvam avagatavān ity āha—naite iti |

sureśā brahmādyā eva māyayā vatsa-bālakākārā ete na bhavanti | na api ṛṣayaḥ | ca-kārān nāpy asurāḥ, kiṁ tu bhidāśraye'pi vividha-bhedāspade’pi vatsa-bālādi-samūhe tvam eva eko bhāsi | ekasyāpi tava pṛthaktvaṁ vatsa-pālādi-rūpatvaṁ sarvaṁ kathaṁ, tan nigamāt saṅkṣepāt vada ity uktena pṛṣṭena prabhuṇā śrī-kṛṣṇena hetunā balaḥ avait brahma-mohanādi-vṛttaṁ jñātavān ||39||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : evaṁ pratyakṣīkṛtyāpi svāminas tasya vatsa-tat-pālaka-rūpatāyāṁ kāraṇaṁ prayojanaṁ ca tam evāpṛcchad ity āha—na ete iti | sureśā viriñcādayo māyayā vatsa-tat-pālā na bhavanti, nāpy ṛṣayaḥ | cād asurāś ca na, kintu bhidāśraye’pi vividha-bheda-kāryavaty asmin vatsa-tat-pāla-vṛnde tvam eva eko bhāsi | ekasyaiva tava pṛthaktvaṁ bheda-kārya-bhāk tvaṁ sarvam idaṁ kathaṁ ? kiṁ-kāraṇakaṁ ? kiṁ-phalakam ? iti nigamāt saṁkṣepena vadety uktena pṛṣṭena prabhuṇā svāminā hetunā balo’vait brahma-mohanādi-vṛttaṁ jñātavān ity arthaḥ ||39||

...

|| 10.13.40 ||

tāvad etyātmabhūr ātma-mānena truṭy-anehasā |

purovad ābdaṁ krīḍantaṁ dadṛśe sa-kalaṁ harim ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : tataḥ kiṁ vṛttam ? ity apekṣāyām āha—tāvad iti | varṣe jāte ātmano mānena truṭy-anehasā truṭi-mātreṇa kālena | sa-kalaṁ sānucaraṁ hariṁ dadarśa ||40||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : punar utsukaḥ pṛcchati—tata iti | tato bala-bodhanāntaram | varṣe samvatsare | ātmano brahmaṇaḥ | mānena pramāṇena | truṭy-anehasā, trasareṇu-trayaṁ bhuṅkte yaḥ kālaḥ sa truṭiḥ smṛtaḥ [bhā.pu. 3.11.6] ity ukta-lakṣaṇa-kālena | ātmabhūr brahmā | purovat pūrvavat | abdaṁ yāvad-varṣam | sānucaraṁ gopa-govat sa-sahitaṁ hariṁ śrī-kṛṣṇam eva | atrāpi baladevo mātrā janmarkṣa-śānty arthaṁ rakṣita iti pūrvavaj jñeyam ||40

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : atha śrī-brahmāpi tat-tattvaṁ śrī-bhagavat-kṛpayaiva jñātavān iti, tat-prasaṅgam ārabhate—tāvad ity-ādinā | tāvad ity avilambaṁ bodhayati | tad evāha—truṭy-anehasā ity atiśīghraṁ gamanaṁ mahā-bhayād iti jñeyam | yata ātmano harer eva bhavatīti, tathā sa tat-putra ity arthaḥ | purovat māyayā svayaṁ vatsādi-haraṇāt pūrvavad ābdam ekābda-paryantam | abdam iti vā pāṭhaḥ | harim iti vakṣyamāṇa-śrī-brahma-citta-haraṇādy-abhiprāyeṇa | sakalā bālā vatsāś ca, na tu śrī-baladevaḥ | tad-dine’pi pūrvavat krīḍā-nirvāhāya vane tad-anānayanāt ||40 ||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : atha brahmāpi tat-tattvaṁ śrī-bhagavat kṛpayaiva jñātavān iti, tat prasaṅga ārabhyate—tāvad ity-ādinā | tāvad iti gate’pi varṣa ity arthaḥ | atiśīghrāgamanaṁ mahā-bhayād iti jñeyam | yata ātmano harer eva bhavatīti, tathā saḥ ābdam ekābda-paryantam | sa-kalam iti kalāḥ bālā vatsāś ca na tu śrī-baladevaḥ tad-dina-krīḍā-nirvāhāya rahasyaṁ kathayitvā vane-tad anayanāt ||40||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : sakalaṁ kalābhir vatsādibhiḥ sahitam ||40||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : prasaṅga eva, gopy-ādīnāṁ mohanādikaṁ vivṛtya | punar brahmaṇo'pi viśeṣato mohanādikaṁ vivarītum ārabhate—tāvad iti | varṣe yāte'pi ātmano mānena truṭy-anehasā truṭi-mātra-kālena | atiśīghrāgamanaṁ mahā-bhayenaiva yata ātmano hareḥ sakāśād eva bhavatīti saḥ ābdam ekābda-paryantaṁ | sakalaṁ vatsa-bālādikaṁ hariṁ kṛṣṇaṁ ca vastutas tu kalās tat svarūpa-bhūtā vatsa-bālādyās tat sahitaṁ dadṛśe dadarśa | baladevas tu pūrva-varṣavat tasminn eva janmarkṣa-dine śāntika-snānādy artha mātrā rakṣita iti pūrvavaj jñeyam ||40||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : tataḥ kim abhūd ? ity atrāha—tāvad iti | varṣe jāte ātmabhūr hari-putra viriñca ātmano mānena truṭy-anehasā truṭi-mātreṇa kālena ety-āgatya | ābdaṁ varṣam ekaṁ vyāpya pūrvavat krīḍantaṁ hariṁ dadarśa | sa-kalaṁ kalā vatsa tat-pālās tat-sahitaṁ | tasmin divase janmarkṣa-yogād balabhadras tan-mātrā gṛha eva śānti-snānādy arthaṁ rakṣita iti bodhyam ||40||

...

|| 10.13.41 ||

yāvanto gokule bālāḥ sa-vatsāḥ sarva eva hi |

māyāśaye śayānā me nādyāpi punar utthitāḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : dṛṣṭvā ca vyatarkayad ity āha—yāvanta iti dvābhyām | me māyāśaye man-māyātalpe ||41||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : vyatarkayad vicāritavān | yāvanto yat pramāṇakāḥ śrī-kṛṣṇa-sahāgatāḥ gokule cāraṇārthā nītavatsa-samūhe | adya api idānīm api vartamāna-dinārthakatve’dyāvyayasya truṭy-anehasety uktyāvirodhaḥ syād iti ||41||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : gokule yāvanto bālā vatsa-pāla-rūpā āsan, tāvantaḥ sarva eva vatsa-sahitāś ca santo | māyety ādi-yojyam ||41||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : gokule yāvanto bālā vatsa-pāla-rūpā āsan, tāvantaḥ sarva eva vatsa-sahitāḥ santo | māyety ādi-yojyam ||41||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : dṛṣṭvā caivaṁ vyatarkayad ity āha—dvābhyām | māyāśaye man-māyātalpe ||41||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : dṛṣṭvā caivaṁ vyatarkayad ity āha yāvanta iti dvābhyām | me māyāśaye man-māyā-śayyāyām ||41||

...

|| 10.13.42 ||

ita ete’tra kutratyā man-māyā-mohitetare |

tāvanta eva tatrābdaṁ krīḍanto viṣṇunā samam ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : man-māyā-mohitebhya itare kutratyāḥ ||42||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : ita iha | saptamy arthe tasiḥ | ete dṛśyamānāḥ | atrety uktis tu sambhramān na teṣāṁ, paunar uktyaṁ śaṅkyam | kutratyāḥ kutaḥ samāgatā ity arthaḥ | tāvantas tat-pramāṇakā eva | yato māyā hṛtās tatrety arthaḥ ||42||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : ita ity asya pūrva-ślokenānvayaḥ, ito māyāśayāt | yad vā, ete māyāśaye śayānā ito vartante | man māyā-mohitetare ca atra kutratyā iti yojyam | ete tu iti pāṭho vā viṣṇuneti padaṁ vyāpakatayaiva krīḍānābhiprāyeṇa ||42||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : ito hetoḥ yad vā ete māyāśaye śayānā ito vartante man māyā-mohitetare ca atra kutratyā iti yojyaṁ | viṣṇunety abdaṁ vāpya, tathaiva krīḍanābhiprāyeṇa ||42||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : ito hetoḥ na kevalam atraiva sthāne | kiṁ tarhi ? abdaṁ vyāpya, tatra gokule ’pi krīḍantaḥ ||42||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : man māyā-mohitās ta eva kṛṣṇenātra-nītā veti vibhāvya | māyikānāṁ nātinikaṭe gatvā tarjanyā sābhinayam āha—itaḥ praveśād | atra kiñcid dūre eve vatsa-bālā vartanta iva | tatra viṣṇunā samaṁ krīḍantaḥ kutratyās te, kīdṛśā man māyā-mohitebhya ? ebhya itare ||42||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : ta eva hariṇā kim ihānītā ? iti vibhāvya | kiñcid dūrāt tān ālocyāha—itaḥ pradeśād | atra kiñcid-dūre man māyāmohitā ete santy eva | tebhya itare tāvanta ete viṣṇunā samaṁ krīḍantaḥ kutratyāḥ ||42||

...

|| 10.13.43 ||

evam eteṣu bhedeṣu ciraṁ dhyātvā sa ātma-bhūḥ |

satyāḥ ke katare neti jñātuṁ neṣṭe kathañcana ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : iti vitarkayann amuhyad ity āha—evam iti dvābhyām | evam eteṣu bhedeṣu ciraṁ dhyātvā sa ātma-bhūḥ iti pāṭhaḥ ||43||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : iti ittham | vitarkayan vimṛśan | bhedeṣu bhinnatvena pratīyamāneṣu gopa-vatseṣu | ātmani cetasi | dhyāna-śabdena cetorthakātma-pada-lābhāt punar ukti-sambhāvanayā pāṭhāntaraṁ caklṛpe—ātmabhūr iti, bhedeṣv iti | kim ete ihaṁ prakṛtās tatrāha—kṛṣṇa-sṛṣṭāḥ | kiṁ vā, eta eva kṛṣṇa-sṛṣṭās tatra te prakṛtāḥ | kiṁ vā, ubhaya eva kṛṣṇa-sṛṣṭāḥ prakṛtās tu kṛṣṇenaiva | kvāpi brahmāṇḍāntare cālitāḥ | kiṁ vā, kṛṣṇena vatsa-pālānāṁ prakāśadvayīkaraṇāt ubhaya eva prakṛtāḥ | kiṁ vā, mayi tatra gatvā tatra paśyati sati eta eva kṛṣṇena tatra nīyante | punar atrāgacchati mayi ta evātra-nīyante |

bhavatu, tarhi yugapad evobhayatra dṛṣṭiṁ nikṣipāmīti | tathā kṛtvāpi tān ubhayatra dṛṣṭvā ciraṁ dhyātvāpi eteṣu madhye bhagavat svarūpa-bhūtāḥ satyā, bahiraṅga-māyā-sṛṣṭāḥ satyā netīmaṁ bhedaṁ tu kathañcana jñātuṁ na śaśāka ||43||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : evam ukta-prakāreṇa bhedeṣu mohitā-mohita-rūpeṣv eteṣu bhayeṣu bahutvaṁ bālakādīnāṁ bāhulyāt | yad vā, bhedena dvidhā vartamāneṣv ity arthaḥ | abhedeṣv iti pāṭhaḥ kvacit, bheda-rahiteṣv ity arthaḥ | eteṣu madhye ke satyāḥ ? katare ca na satyāḥ ? kiṁ nāma man-mohitān ? bhagavan nītvā tat-parivartenānye tādṛśa-māyayā nirmāyātrāmī sthāpitāḥ | kiṁ vā, bhagavatā saha krīḍanta ete māyā-nirmitāḥ santīti | kiṁ vā, māyā bhrāntyaiva te dṛśyante ete | veti ciraṁ dhyātvāpi nija-vicārādi-prayāsena niścetuṁ na śakta ity arthaḥ | sa māyā vistārako'py ātmabhūḥ svataḥ-siddha-jñāno'pīty arthaḥ ||43||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : evam ukta-prakāreṇa bhedeṣu mohitānusandhānaṁ amohitānusandhānaṁ tayor viparyayas teṣu | eteṣu madhye ke satyāḥ anāropita-pūrva-vatsa-bāla-bhāvāḥ katare ca na satyāḥ | kiṁ nāma man-mohitān ? bhagavatā nītvā tat-parivartenānye | tādṛśā māyayā nirmāyātrāpy amī sthāpitāḥ | kiṁ vā, bhagavatā saha krīḍanta ete māyā-nirmitāḥ | kiṁ vā, mayā bhrāntyaiva te dṛśyanta ete | veti ciraṁ dhyātvāpi nija-vicārādi-prayāsena niścetuṁ neṣṭe, na śakta ity arthaḥ | sa māyā-vistārako’pi ātma-bhūḥ svataḥ siddha-jñāno’pi bhagavan māyayaiva jñātuṁ neṣṭe ity arthaḥ ||43||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : eteṣu bhedeśv iti kim etat iha prakṛtās tatrāha—kṛṣṇa-sṛṣṭāḥ | kiṁ vā, eta eva kṛṣṇa-sṛṣṭās tatra te prakṛtāḥ | kiṁ vā, ubhaye eva kṛṣṇa-sṛṣṭāḥ prakṛtās tu kṛṣṇenaiva | kvāpi brahmāṇḍāntare cālitāḥ | kiṁ vā, kṛṣṇena vatsa-bālānāṁ prakāśa-dvayī-karaṇāt ubhaye eva | kiṁ vā, mayi tatra gatvā paśyati sati eta eva kṛṣṇena tatra nīyante | punar atrāgacchati mayi ta evātra nīyante |

bhavatu, tarhi yugapad evobhayatra dṛṣṭīr nikṣipāmīti | tathā kṛtvāpi tān ubhayatra dṛṣṭvā ciraṁ dhyātveti bhavatu, svīya-sarvajñatayaivāham avaśyaṁ jñāsyāmīti | bahusamādhināpi jñātuṁ naivāśakad ity āha—satyā iti | eteṣu bhedeṣu madhye satyā bhagavat-svarūpa-bhūtā na satyā, bahiraṅga-māyā-sṛṣṭā itī me bhedaṁ tu, kathañcana jñātuṁ saṁśaya-jñāna-viṣayīkartum api neṣṭe na śaśāka ||43||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : atha viriñcasya viśiṣṭaṁ moham āha—evam iti | kim ihaitā ? prakṛtās tatra te kṛṣṇena nirmitāḥ | kiṁ vā, eta eva te na nirmitās tatra te prakṛtāḥ | kiṁ vobhaye’pi tena racitāḥ, man-nītāḥ prakṛtās tu tenānyatra | kvāpi cālitā ity evam eteṣu bhedeṣu viśeṣeṣu, bhedā dvaidhe viśeṣe syād iti medinī | ciraṁ dhyātvā datta-dṛṣṭiḥ san-nibhālya satyāḥ prakṛtāḥ | ke ? katare ca neti tena kuhakinaiva racitāḥ ke vety arthaḥ | iti kathañcana kenāpi prakāreṇa saṁśaya-jñānenāpi jñātuṁ neṣṭena śaśāka | kintu vimohito’bhūd ity arthaḥ ||43||

...

|| 10.13.44 ||

evaṁ sammohayan viṣṇuṁ vimohaṁ viśva-mohanam |

svayaiva māyayājo’pi svayam eva vimohitaḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : vimohaṁ mohān adhikaraṇam | viśva-mohanaṁ sarva-mohakaram | ajo’pīty apinā brahmaṇo’pi yadā mohas tadā kāny eṣāṁ katheti dhvanitam | svayā svakṛtayā svasya harer vā, ātmātmīyayoḥ svaḥ iti kośāntarāt | mohitasyāpi brahmaṇa eva vihvalīkaraṇe bhagavati māyā-prayoga-rūpāparādha eva hetur iti bhāvaḥ ||44||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : sammohayan sva-māyayā vatsādy-ācchadanāt | yad vā, sammohayitum icchann ity arthaḥ | evaṁ tad abhiprāyānusāreṇaiva saṁ-śabdaḥ | viṣṇum iti sarva-vyāpakam—māyayā tasya dṛṣṭy-ācchādanaṁ na ghaṭeteti bhāvaḥ | ato vimohaṁ mohayitum aśakyam apīty arthaḥ | kiṁ ca, viśva-mohanampy ajaḥ svayam bhūr iti pūrvavat eva śabdābhyām | na tu bhagavan māyayā, na tu bhagavān veti bodhyate ||44||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : sammohayan sva-māyayā vatsādy-ācchādanāt sammohatum icchann ity arthaḥ | evaṁ tad abhiprāyānusāreṇaiva saṁ-śabdaḥ | viṣṇum iti, sarva-vyāpakatvān—māyayā tasya dṛṣṭy ācchādanaṁ na ghaṭeteti bhāvaḥ | ato vimohaṁ mohayitum aśakyam apīty arthaḥ | kiṁ viśva-mohanam apy ajaḥ svayambhūr ? iti, pūrvavat eva-śabdābhyāṁ na tu bhagavan māyayā, na tu bhagavān veti bodhyate ||44||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : mohitānusandhānam amohitānusandhānaṁ tayor viparyaya | evaṁ prakāreṣv eteṣu bhedeṣu anāropita-pūrvavat | sa-bāla-bhāvāḥ māyā-hatānāṁ vā kathaṁ mad-vañcanāya bhagavan-māyā-kalpitatvam iti bhāvayitvā iti bhāvaḥ ||44||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : tataś ca brahmā moha-samudrāvarte nipapātety āha—evam iti | saṁmohayan vatsa-bālas te yena mohayitum upakramamāṇaḥ ajo brahmāpi | svayaiva māyayā svayam eva viṣṇau prayuktayā hetunā vimohitaḥ | bhagavan-māyayā viśeṣeṇaiva mohitaḥ mohitasyāpi brahmaṇa | evaṁ vihvalīkaraṇa-rūpe vimohane bhagavati māyā-prayoga-rūpo'parādha eva kāraṇam ity arthaḥ | na tu sva-māyayaiva brahmā vimohita iti vyākhyeyam | māyāyāḥ svāśraya-vyāmohakatvāsambhavāt uttara-śloke dṛṣānta-virodhāc ca ||44||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : spuṭayati evaṁ vatsa-tat-pāla-cauryeṇa viṣṇuṁ nandārbhakaṁ mohayan ajo viriñco’pi svayaiva māyayā | tasmin prayuktayā hetunā tat-prayogāparādhena kāraṇena viṣṇos tasya māyayā viśeṣeṇaiva mohitaḥ | na ca sva-māyayaiva svayaṁ vimohita iti vyākhyeyaṁ | māyāyāḥ svāśrayāvyāmohakatvāt parapadyokta-dṛṣṭānta-virodhāc ca | kīdṛśam ity āha—vimohaṁ vigato moho yasmāt tam | māyā-nivartakaṁ jñāna-padaṁ viśveṣāṁ svetareṣāṁ sarveṣāṁ vimohanaṁ yogayā triguṇayā ca yathāyathaṁ mohakaram ity arthaḥ ||44||

...

|| 10.13.45 ||

tamyāṁ tamovan naihāraṁ khadyotārcir ivāhani |

mahatītara-māyaiśyaṁ nihanty ātmani yuñjataḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : nanu, māyāśrayasya tasya kuto mohaḥ ? ity ata āha—tamyām iti | mahā-māyāvini bhagavaty anya-māyāyā āvaraṇa-vikṣepakatvābhāve dṛṣṭānta-dvayam itam | tamyāṁ tamisrāyāṁ rātryāṁ naihāraṁ hima-kaṇa-prabhavaṁ tama iva | taddhitārthopasarjanasyāpi tamaso naihāram iti viśeṣaṇaṁ chāndasam | tat tamo yathā pṛthag āvaraṇaṁ na karoti, kintu tatraiva līyate | yathā ca khadyotārcir ahani pṛthak prakāśaṁ na karoti | evaṁ mahati puruṣe māyāṁ yuñjataḥ puṁsa itarā nīcā māyā tatra na kiñcit karoti, kintv ātmani svasminn eva aiśyaṁ sāmarthyaṁ nihantīti ||45||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : māyāyāḥ svāśravyāmohakatvasyendra-jāliya-māyā-dṛṣṭāntena prasiddhatvād-brahmaṇo’pi māyāśratvān moho na ghaṭata ity āśaṅkate—nanv ity-ādinā | rajanī yāminī tamī iti kośāt sādhāraṇa-rātriparo’pi tamī-śabda ihārtha-svārasyāt tamisrāparo jñeyaḥ | tathā ca tamyāṁ tamoyuktāyāṁ rātrau | mahā-māyā-vinīti | māyinām api māyinī ity ukteḥ | anya-māyāyā bhagavad itara-māyāyāḥ | āvaraṇa-vikṣepa-śaktidvaya-viśiṣṭā bhagavata eva māyā nānyasyeti bhāvaḥ | tamasā tulyaṁ tamovad iti tulyārthe vatis taddhitaḥ | viśeṣyaṁ tu pradhānaṁ syāt ity ukteḥ | prādhānyam atra taddhitārthasyaiva na tu tamasaḥ | tathā ca katham atra tamaso viśeṣyatvaṁ ? tad abhāve naihāram iti | viśeṣaṇaṁ katham ? tatrāha—chāndasam iti | yad vā, vadavyayam ivārthesti | tatra mahati ||45||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : tamyām ity-ādi | māyā tāvad dvedhā—āvarikā prakāśikā ca | ubhayatra dhyāyaḥ—āvarikāyāṁ tamyām iti | ahanīti prakāśikāyām | iva-śabda ubhayatra yojyaḥ | naihāraṁ nīhāra-samūhaḥ, tasya viśeṣaṇaṁ tamovat tamo-yuktaṁ, tamovat rakṣad iti vā ||45||

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tac cocitam evety āha—tamyām iti | naihāra-tamasaḥ kadā drāghyāndhakāra naiviḍyāpādanena kiñcit prayojana-siddhyā dṛṣṭāntāntaram āha—khadyoteti | sūryāṁśu-prabhāveṇa khadyotasya khedas tad arciṣaś ca nāśa eva yathā, tad vad ity arthaḥ | itarā itarasya vā māyā, tāṁ yuñjato māyāṁ prayuñjataḥ ||45||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tac cocitam evety āha—tamyām iti | yad atra ṭīkāyām āvaraṇa-vikṣepa-kramaḥ sammataḥ tad anusṛtya vyākhyāyate | tamyāṁ naihāraṁ tamo yathā tamī nāvṛṇoti | kiṁ ca, tac ca svayam eva līnaṁsat tamī tamaḥ sāndrīkṛtya nīhāram evāvṛṇoti yathā | cāhanyahaḥ sthitasya pūrṇasya candrasyārcir api sūryārciḥ svatayā pratyāyayati tathā | khadyotārci-kartṛ-suryīrcir api svatayā pratyāyayituṁ na śaknoti | kiṁ tu, khadyotam eva pratihata-prabhāvatvena jñāpayati | tadvan mahati māyāṁ prayuñjānasyetarasya māyāmahac chaktim āvarītuṁ bhāvāntaraṁ ca vikṣeptum asamarthā satī svāśrayatayātmatvena vyapadiśya | tasmin itaratra yad aiśyan-tad eva nihantīty arthaḥ | avyayam api vac-chabdo’sti | yad vā, yathā tathaivaivaṁ sāmya ity amaraḥ ||45||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bhagavat-sandarbhaḥ 19) : ātma-māyā svarūpa-śaktiḥ | mīyate’nayeti māyā-śabdena śakti-mātram api bhaṇyate—tasyām iti, brahma-vākyaṁ tathaiva saṅgacchate | śakti-mātrasya tāratamyaṁ hi tatra vivakṣitam | svalpā śaktiḥ khalv anṛtasya satyasya vā vyañjikā bhavatu nāma, parābhavāya kalpata eveti hi tatra gamyate | dṛṣṭāntābhyāṁ ca tathaiva prakaṭitaṁ tasyāṁ tamovad ity-ādibhyām | tathā yuddheṣu māyāmaya-śastrādinā bahavaś chinna-bhinnā jātā iti purāṇādiṣu śrūyate ||45||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : itarasya kṣudrasya māyā sva-prabhāva-darśanā tāṁ yuñjataḥ puṁsaḥ ātmani aiśyaṁ hanti ||45||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : atha durghaṭa-ghaṭana-paṭīyasī śaktir māyā | sā ca

dvividhā—māyayā bahu-rūpayā ity uktatvāt | tatra prathamaṁ dvedhā—prakāśikā āvarikā ca | ādyā ca dvedhā—svābhāvikī aicchikī ca | ubhe evānanda-śaktitvāt prakāśike | dvitīyā tu svabhāva-saṅkocinī kuhakaṁ ceti | ubhayor āvaraṇa-dharmatvād āvarikātvam | tatra prakāśikāvarikayor māyayor akrameṇa dṛṣṭāntam āha—tamyāṁ tamovad ity-ādi | tamyām ity āvarikāyāṁ dhyāyaḥ, ahanīti prakāśikāyām | mahā-māyika-cakra-cūḍāmaṇau tatra kvāpareṣāṁ māyā | yatra brahmaṇo’pi māyaivam āsīd ity āśayaḥ | naihāraṁ nīhāra-samūhaḥ | kīdṛśaḥ ? tamovat tamo’rakṣat [kīdṛśaḥ ! ghanaśyāmāḥ pīta-kauśeya-vāsasaḥ] pāṭha bhedaḥ ||45.53||

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : mahā-māyāvini bhagavaty-anya-māyā āvaraṇa-vikṣepau kartum aśaknuvatī | svāśrayam eva tiraskarotīti dṛṣṭāntābhyām āha—tamyāntām asyāṁ rātrau | naihāraṁ tamovat nīhāra-sambandhi tama iva ivārthe'tra vac-chabdaḥ, iva vad vā ca sādṛśye ity abhidhānāt | naihāraṁ tamo yathā tamīm āvarītum asamarthaṁ, tamī tamaḥ sāndrīkṛtya tena svam evāvṛṇoti nīhāraṁ ca tiraskaroti | tathaiva brahma-māyā bhagavantaṁ mohayitum asamarthā | bhagavad aiśvaryam eva vipulīkṛtya svramāvṛtavatīṁ brahmāṇam eva | tiraś cakāreti-dṛṣṭānte'sminn aṁśena brahma-māyāyā api hetutvam astīty aparituṣyan dṛṣṭāntaram āha—svadyoteti | rātrau yathā pradyotate, tathā divase'pi mat-prabhā pradyotatām iti khadyotena prayuktāpi prabhā divase udbhavitum eva na śaknoti | pratyuta tam eva bhraṣṭa-tejasaṁ sarvān jñāpayati | tathaivānyatraiśvaryavān api brahmā bhagavaty api māyayā nijaiśvaryaṁ prakaṭayitu-kāmo bhraṣṭa-tejā evābhūd ity ataḥ | mahati puruṣe itara-māyā-katrī ātmani ātmānaṁ yuñjata svaṁ prayuñjānasya puṁsaḥ aiśyam aiśvaryaṁ nihanti ||45||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : mahāmāyini harau tad anya-māyā na prabhavati, kintu svāśrayam eva tiraskaroti iti | dṛṣṭāntābhyām āha—tasyāṁ tamisrāyāṁ niśi naihāraṁ tamovat ivārthe’tra vac-chadaḥ, iva vad vā ca sādṛśye ity ukteḥ | himakaṇa-prabhavaṁ tamo yathā tamīm āvarītum asamarthaṁ | tat-tamo niviḍayan-nihāram eva tiraskaroti | tathā viriñca-māyā hariṁ mohayitum asamarthā | tasyaiśvaryam eva vardhayantī viriñcam eva tiraskarotīty asmin dṛṣṭānte | tan-māyāyā hetutvam astīty asatoṣād-dṛṣṭāntāntaram āha—khadyota-prayuktārcir yathā ahani pṛthak prakāśaṁ na karotīti, kitnu tatraiva vilīyate khadyotas tu bhraṣṭa-tejāḥ pratīyate | tathā mahati puruṣe yuñjataḥ puṁsa itarā tucchā māyā ātmani sthitam aiśyaṁ nihantīti viriñcasya māyā vinaṣṭā sa ca bhraṣṭa-tejā abhūd iti bhāvaḥ ||45||

...

|| 10.13.46-47 ||

tāvat sarve vatsa-pālāḥ paśyato’jasya tat-kṣaṇāt |

vyadṛśyanta ghana-śyāmāḥ pīta-kauśeya-vāsasaḥ ||

catur-bhujāḥ śaṅkha-cakra-gadā-rājīva-pāṇayaḥ |

kirīṭinaḥ kuṇḍalino hāriṇo vana-mālinaḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : anyad api āścaryam āha—tāvad iti | vatsa-pālāḥ vatsāḥ pālāś ca | sarve yaṣṭi-viṣāṇādayaś ca ||46-47||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : ato’py adbhutam āhu—tāvat tadā sakalā vā, sarva-śabdasya yaṣṭy-ādi-paratvena vyākhyānāt vyadṛśyanta brahmaṇeti śeṣaḥ ||46|| atra viśvanāthaḥ,

rājīvaṁ kamalam, bisa-prasūnarājīva ity amaraḥ ||47||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : pūrvaṁ vatsa-pāla-rūpa evābhavat, samprati tad-ākāratāṁ tirodhāpya svarūpeṇaivāvirabhūd iti darśayati—tāvat sarva ity-ādi ||46||

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : evaṁ mohena dīnatāṁ gate śrī-brahmaṇi śrī-bhagavān acirāt kṛpāṁ vyatanod ity āha—tāvad ity-ādinā | kartari ṣaṣṭhī | paśyatā punar api vicārayatety arthaḥ | kiṁ vā, dainyena bhayena ca śrī-bhagavantaṁ nirīkṣyamāṇena | yad vā, tān eva paśyatāpi satā | ity āścarya-viśeṣa ukto bhramādikaṁ ca nirastam | viśeṣato’dṛśyanta dṛṣṭāḥ | yad vā, aje paśyati sati vyadṛśyanta dṛṣṭi-viṣayā jātāḥ ||46||

caturbhujatvāikaṁ śrī-vaikuṇṭha-nāthatva-bodhanārtham tat sevakasya śrī-brahmaṇas tathaiva tan-māhātmya-pratīteḥ | yad vā, brahmādibhiḥ śrī-kṛṣṇasya prāyaś catur-bhūjādi rūpeṇaiva darśanāt ||47||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : evaṁ mohena dīnatāṁ gate brahmaṇi śrī-bhagavān apy acirātāt draṣṭuṁ mañju-mahitvam anyad api yad | iti tad abhiprāyānusāreṇaiva kṛpāṁ vyatanod ity āha—tāvad ity-ādi-navakena | aṅkās tu pṛthak pṛthak kriyante paśyantam ajam anādṛtya tad dṛṣṭi-śaktim anapekṣya adṛśyanta | svayam eva tad dṛṣṭau vyaktī-bhūtāḥ sva-śakti mātreṇābhivyakteḥ karma-kartṛtvam ||46||

caturbhujā ity atra caturbhujatvādinā viṣṇutvam avagamyate | māyādy adhiṣṭhātṛtvena tu prathama dvitīya-puruṣatvam avagaṁsyate tasmāt—

sṛjāmi tan niyukto’haṁ haro harati tad vaśaḥ |

viśvaṁ puruṣa-rūpeṇa paripāti triśakti-dhṛk ||

iti brahma-vākyāt brahmāṇaṁ prati ca tat-tat-kāryāya sarva-śakti-vyañjakatayā prāyo viṣṇor evāvirbhāva-śravaṇāt trayāṇām abheda-jñāpanārtham eva vyāmiśratvenāvir bhāvo’yaṁ jñeyaḥ ||47||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : kīdṛśaḥ ? ghana-śyāmāḥ pīta-kauśeya-vāsasaḥ ||46||

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : tāvad iti | yāvad evaṁ brahmā mīmāṁsamāno vyāmuhyati smety arthaḥ | vatsā pālāś ca paśyato’jasya paśyantam apy ajam anādṛtyeti | bhoḥ satya-loka-vāsin aja ! satyaṁ tvam aja evāsi īdṛśyaiva buddhyā viśvaṁ sṛjasi asmān māyayā mohayitum icchasi | kathañcit jñātum api tāvan na śaknoṣi paśyeti vyadṛśyanta, vayaṁ vṛndāvanīyās tṛṇaṁ caranto vatsā api vatsāṁś cārayanto gopa-bālā api evaṁ bhavāmeti jñāpayanta iva | tad-dṛṣṭi-gocarāḥ svayam evābhuvan sva-prakāśatvād iti bhāvaḥ ||46-47||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : sāparādhe’pi viriñca-bhṛtyatvāt kṛpaiva nandārbhakasyāsīd ity āha—tāvad iti | yāvad evaṁ vicārayan vimuhyati smety arthaḥ | vatsāt tat-pālāś ca sarve ghana-śyāmādi-lakṣaṇā vyadṛśyanta karmakartari prayogaḥ | sva-prakāśatvāt svayam eva dṛṣṭi-gocarā abhūvan | paśyato’jasyety anādare ṣaṣṭhī | bho satyaloka-nivāsin aja ! tvam anayaiva budhyā viśvaṁ sṛjasi ? svāminy api nandārbhake māyāṁ tanoṣi | tad aṁśānapy asmān vṛndāṭavī nivāsinaḥ kathañcid api jñātuṁ neṣṭe ! paśyāsmākaṁ mahaiśvaryāṇy asmābhir eva darśitānīti ||46.47||

...

|| 10.13.48 ||

śrīvatsāṅgada-do-ratna-kambu-kaṅkaṇa-pāṇayaḥ |

nūpuraiḥ kaṭakair bhātāḥ kaṭi-sūtrāṅgulīyakaiḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : śrīvatsa-prabhā-yuktān yad aṅgāni doḥṣu yeṣām, ratnamayāni kambu-vat tri-dhārāṇi kaṅkaṇāni pāṇiṣu yeṣāṁ, te ca te ca ||48||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : aṅgadāni keyūrāṇi | aṅgadaṁ bāhu-bhūṣāyām aṅgado bālije’pi ca iti dharaṇiḥ | kaṭako valayo’striyām ity ukteḥ, kara-bhūṣaṇam api kaṭako’tra nūpura-sāhacaryāt pāda-bhūṣaṇaparaḥ | kaṭi-sūtrāṇi kṣutra-ghaṇṭikā-yuktāni sūtra-protakaṭi-bhūṣaṇāni ||48||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) :na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : śrīvatso nāma dakṣiṇa-stanordhe sūkṣma-romṇāṁ dakṣiṇāvartaḥ śrī-bhagavato'sādhāraṇa-lakṣaṇam tena ca sārūpya-prāpta-pārṣada-sādṛśyaṁ nirastam | kaṭakaiḥ pāda-valayaiḥ ||48||

———————————————————————————————————————
